--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/acesso-rapido/AtualizacaoCadastralAccordion.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { UserCog, Mail, Phone } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../../../api';

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
}

export function AtualizacaoCadastralAccordion(_props: Props) {
  const [contactLoading, setContactLoading] = useState(false);
  const [contactEmail, setContactEmail] = useState("");
  const [contactPhone, setContactPhone] = useState("");
  const [contactFeedback, setContactFeedback] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error', message: string } | null>(null);

  const handleUpdateContact = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setContactFeedback(null);

    if (!contactEmail.trim() && !contactPhone.trim()) {
      setContactFeedback({ type: 'error', message: 'Por favor, preencha pelo menos o e-mail ou o telefone.' });
      return;
    }

    setContactLoading(true);
    try {
      await api.usuarios.atualizarContato(contactEmail, contactPhone);
      setContactFeedback({ type: 'success', message: 'Dados de contato atualizados com sucesso!' });
      setContactEmail("");
      setContactPhone("");
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(error);
      setContactFeedback({ type: 'error', message: 'Erro ao atualizar contato. Tente novamente.' });
    } finally {
      setContactLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e7eb] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm flex flex-col h-full">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-[24px] pb-4 border-b border-gray-100">
        <div className="w-12 h-[48px] rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          <UserCog className="w-6 h-[24px]" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#1f2937]">
            Atualização Cadastral
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
            Mantenha seus dados em dia
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="p-[24px] flex-1 flex flex-col">
        {contactFeedback && (
          <div
            role={contactFeedback.type === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
            aria-live="polite"
            className={`mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium ${
              contactFeedback.type === 'success' 
                ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200' 
                : 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200'
            }`}
          >
            {contactFeedback.message}
          </div>
        )}
        <form onSubmit={handleUpdateContact} className="flex flex-col gap-4 mt-auto">
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-100">
            <strong>Atenção:</strong> A alteração de e-mail nesta seção atualiza apenas o seu e-mail de contato para notificações. O seu e-mail de acesso (login) permanece o mesmo.
          </p>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true" />
            <input id="contactEmail" name="contactEmail"
              type="email"
              aria-label="Novo E-mail de Contato"
              placeholder="Novo E-mail de Contato"
              value={contactEmail}
              onChange={(e) => setContactEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-11 pr-3 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors bg-gray-50/50 focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="relative">
            <Phone className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true" />
            <input id="contactPhone" name="contactPhone"
              type="tel"
              aria-label="Telefone ou WhatsApp"
              placeholder="Telefone / WhatsApp"
              value={contactPhone}
              onChange={(e) => setContactPhone(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-11 pr-3 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors bg-gray-50/50 focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={contactLoading}
            className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white py-3 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-[#1e40af] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 mt-2"
          >
            {contactLoading ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar Dados"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/acesso-rapido/AtualizacaoCadastralAccordion.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/acesso-rapido/CentralAjudaAccordion.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Headset, Send, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../../../api';

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
}

export function CentralAjudaAccordion(_props: Props) {
  const [supportLoading, setSupportLoading] = useState(false);
  const [supportSubject, setSupportSubject] = useState("");
  const [supportMessage, setSupportMessage] = useState("");
  const [supportSent, setSupportSent] = useState(false);
  const [supportError, setSupportError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSendSupport = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!supportSubject.trim() || !supportMessage.trim()) return;

    setSupportLoading(true);
    setSupportError(null);
    try {
      await api.suporte.enviarMensagem({ assunto: supportSubject, mensagem: supportMessage });
      setSupportSent(true);
      setSupportSubject("");
      setSupportMessage("");
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao enviar suporte:", error);
      setSupportError(error.message || "Falha de conexão.");
    } finally {
      setSupportLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e7eb] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm flex flex-col h-full">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-[24px] pb-4 border-b border-gray-100">
        <div className="w-12 h-[48px] rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          <Headset className="w-6 h-[24px]" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#1f2937]">
            Central de Ajuda
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
            Fale com nossa equipe
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-[24px] flex-1 flex flex-col">
        {supportSent ? (
          <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center flex-1 py-[16px]">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-green-100 flex items-center justify-center mb-[16px]">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-green-600" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-900 mb-2">Mensagem enviada</h4>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 max-w-[200px]">
              Nossa equipe de suporte entrará em contato em breve.
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSupportSent(false)}
              className="mt-6 text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:underline"
            >
              Enviar outra mensagem
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSendSupport} className="flex flex-col gap-4 mt-auto">
            {supportError && (
              <div role="alert" className="bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm p-3 rounded-lg border border-red-100">
                {supportError}
              </div>
            )}
            <select
              id="support-subject"
              aria-label="Assunto do chamado"
              value={supportSubject}
              onChange={(e) => setSupportSubject(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors bg-gray-50/50 focus:bg-white"
            >
              <option value="" disabled>Selecione um assunto...</option>
              <option value="Dúvida sobre cursos">Dúvida sobre cursos</option>
              <option value="Dúvida sobre vagas">Dúvida sobre vagas</option>
              <option value="Problemas com certificado">Problemas com certificado</option>
              <option value="Problemas de acesso">Problemas de acesso</option>
              <option value="Outros">Outros</option>
            </select>
            <textarea
              id="support-message"
              aria-label="Mensagem para a Central de Ajuda"
              placeholder="Como podemos te ajudar?"
              value={supportMessage}
              onChange={(e) => setSupportMessage(e.target.value)}
              required
              rows={3}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-300 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors resize-none bg-gray-50/50 focus:bg-white"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={supportLoading}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white py-3 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-[#1e40af] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 mt-2"
            >
              {supportLoading ? (
                "Enviando..."
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Enviar Mensagem
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/acesso-rapido/CentralAjudaAccordion.tsx ---

import { Award, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/CarteiraEstudanteHero.tsx ---
import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '../../api';
import { useAuthStore, getAuthSessionSnapshot, isAuthSessionCurrent } from '../../store/useAuthStore';
import { useExternalLinkStore } from '../../store/useExternalLinkStore';

interface CarteiraEstudanteHeroProps {
  firstName: string;
  onDismissSession: () => void;
}
export function CarteiraEstudanteHero({ firstName, onDismissSession }: CarteiraEstudanteHeroProps) {
  const { setUserData } = useAuthStore();
  const { openLink } = useExternalLinkStore();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const handleMarkAsOwned = async () => {
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      await api.usuarios.marcarCarteiraComoObtida();
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      const currentUserData = useAuthStore.getState().userData;
      if (currentUserData) {
        setUserData({ ...currentUserData, statusCarteira: 'JA_TENHO' });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Erro ao atualizar status da carteira:', error);
    } finally {
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="relative rounded-[16px] bg-gradient-to-r from-[#1e3a8a] to-[#1e40af] p-[24px] sm:p-[32px] lg:p-[40px] shadow-[0_10px_15px_-3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1),_0_4px_6px_-4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] flex items-center">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] pointer-events-none">
        <div 
          className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 rounded-full bg-white/5"
          style={{ transform: 'translateY(-50%) translateX(33.333333%)' }}
        />
        <div 
          className="absolute bottom-0 right-32 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-white/5"
          style={{ transform: 'translateY(33.333333%) translateX(25%)' }}
        />
      </div>
      <div className="relative z-10 w-full flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-8">
        <div className="w-full md:max-w-2xl lg:max-w-3xl">
          <h1 className="text-2xl lg:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2 md:whitespace-nowrap">
            Carteira de Estudante da Administração
          </h1>
          <p className="text-lg text-[#dbeafe] leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-xl">
            Olá, {firstName}! Garanta já a sua Carteira de Estudante oficial do CRA-SP.<br/>
            É 100% gratuita, digital e cheia de benefícios.
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
            <button 
              onClick={() => openLink('https://crasp.gov.br/estudante/carteira/', 'CRA-SP')}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-yellow-500 border border-transparent text-black py-2.5 px-6 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-yellow-400"
            >
              Eu Quero
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={handleMarkAsOwned}
              disabled={loading}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 border border-white/20 text-white py-2.5 px-6 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-white/20 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Atualizando...' : 'Já tenho'}
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={onDismissSession}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-transparent border border-white/40 text-white/80 py-2.5 px-6 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-white/10 hover:border-white/60 hover:text-white"
            >
              Ver depois
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <img 
        src="/image-carteiraestudante.webp?v=2" 
        alt="Carteira de Estudante CRA-SP" 
        className="absolute right-4 -bottom-0 h-[100%] md:h-[115%] object-contain object-right-bottom hidden md:block z-20 pointer-events-none"
        style={{ maxWidth: '40%' }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/CursosFilterBar.tsx ---

export type CursosFilterBarProps = Readonly<{
  filtroInstituicao: string;
  setFiltroInstituicao: (val: string) => void;
  instituicoes: readonly string[];
  filtroArea: string;
  setFiltroArea: (val: string) => void;
  areas: readonly string[];
  filtroCarga: string;
  setFiltroCarga: (val: string) => void;
  cargasDisponiveis: ReadonlySet<string>;
}>;

const RANGES = [
  { id: "ate_4", label: "ATÉ 4 HORAS" },
  { id: "ate_12", label: "DE 5 A 12 HORAS" },
  { id: "ate_20", label: "DE 13 A 20 HORAS" },
  { id: "ate_40", label: "DE 21 A 40 HORAS" },
  { id: "ate_80", label: "DE 41 A 80 HORAS" },
  { id: "acima_80", label: "ACIMA DE 80 HORAS" },
];

export function CursosFilterBar({
  filtroInstituicao,
  setFiltroInstituicao,
  instituicoes,
  filtroArea,
  setFiltroArea,
  areas,
  filtroCarga,
  setFiltroCarga,
  cargasDisponiveis,
}: CursosFilterBarProps) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-8">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <label htmlFor="filtro-cursos-instituicao" className="text-xs uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 tracking-widest mb-2">
            Instituição
          </label>
          <select
            id="filtro-cursos-instituicao"
            className="border border-[#d1d5db] bg-white text-[#374151] px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2563eb]/20 focus:border-[#2563eb] uppercase text-sm transition-all shadow-sm hover:border-[#9ca3af]"
            value={filtroInstituicao}
            onChange={(e) => setFiltroInstituicao(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">TODAS AS INSTITUIÇÕES</option>
            {instituicoes.map((inst) => (
              <option key={inst} value={inst}>
                {inst}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <label htmlFor="filtro-cursos-area" className="text-xs uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 tracking-widest mb-2">
            Área
          </label>
          <select
            id="filtro-cursos-area"
            className="border border-[#d1d5db] bg-white text-[#374151] px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2563eb]/20 focus:border-[#2563eb] uppercase text-sm transition-all shadow-sm hover:border-[#9ca3af]"
            value={filtroArea}
            onChange={(e) => setFiltroArea(e.target.value)}
            disabled={!filtroInstituicao && areas.length === 0}
          >
            <option value="">TODAS AS ÁREAS</option>
            {areas.map((area) => (
              <option key={area} value={area}>
                {area}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <label htmlFor="filtro-cursos-carga" className="text-xs uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 tracking-widest mb-2">
            Carga Horária
          </label>
          <select
            id="filtro-cursos-carga"
            className="border border-[#d1d5db] bg-white text-[#374151] px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2563eb]/20 focus:border-[#2563eb] uppercase text-sm transition-all shadow-sm hover:border-[#9ca3af]"
            value={filtroCarga}
            onChange={(e) => setFiltroCarga(e.target.value)}
            disabled={
              !filtroArea &&
              !filtroInstituicao &&
              cargasDisponiveis.size === 0
            }
          >
            <option value="">QUALQUER CARGA HORÁRIA</option>
            {RANGES.filter((r) => cargasDisponiveis.has(r.id)).map((r) => (
              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                {r.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/FaqAccordion.tsx ---
import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { useHomeStore } from '../../store/useHomeStore';
import { useInView } from '../../hooks/useInView';

interface FaqItemProps {
  index: number;
  question: string;
  answer: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}

function FaqItem({ index, question, answer, isOpen, onClick }: FaqItemProps) {
  const panelId = `faq-panel-${index}`;
  return (
    <div className="border-b border-[#e5e7eb] last:border-b-0">
      <button 
        type="button"
        onClick={onClick}
        className="flex items-center justify-between w-full py-6 group text-left"
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        aria-controls={panelId}
      >
        <span className="text-base font-semibold text-[#111827] pr-4 group-hover:text-[#2563eb] transition-colors">
          {question}
        </span>
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#f9fafb] group-hover:bg-[#eff6ff] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-colors">
          <ChevronDown 
            className={`w-5 h-[20px] text-[#6b7280] group-hover:text-[#2563eb] transition-transform duration-300 ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
        </div>
      </button>
      
      <div 
        id={panelId}
        className={`transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden ${
          isOpen ? 'max-h-[24rem] opacity-100 pb-6' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0'
        }`}
      >
        <p className="text-sm text-[#4b5563] leading-[1.625]">
          {answer}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function FaqAccordion() {
  const [openIndex, setOpenIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
  const { perguntas, loading } = useHomeStore();
  const ref = useRef(null);
  useInView(ref, { once: true, margin: "300px 0px" });

  if (perguntas.length === 0 && !loading) {
    return null;
  }

  const visibleFaqs = isExpanded ? perguntas : perguntas.slice(0, 3);
  const hasMore = perguntas.length > 3;

  const handleToggleExpand = () => {
    if (isExpanded) setOpenIndex(null);
    setIsExpanded(!isExpanded);
  };

  return (
    <section ref={ref}  className="w-full bg-[#f9fafb] py-16 md:py-24 min-h-[500px]">
      <div 
        className="max-w-[896px] mx-auto px-[16px]"
      >
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] text-center mb-12">
          Dúvidas Frequentes
        </h2>
        
        <div className="bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-6 md:p-8">
          {visibleFaqs.map((faq, index) => (
            <FaqItem 
              key={index}
              index={index}
              question={faq.pergunta}
              answer={faq.resposta}
              isOpen={openIndex === index}
              onClick={() => setOpenIndex(openIndex === index ? null : index)}
            />
          ))}
          
          {hasMore && (
            <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
              <button
                onClick={handleToggleExpand}
                className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[rgba(239,246,255,0.5)] border border-[#dbeafe] py-[10px] px-6 text-sm font-medium text-[#1e40af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#eff6ff] hc-border hc-no-underline"
                aria-expanded={isExpanded}
              >
                {isExpanded ? 'Ver menos perguntas' : 'Ver mais perguntas'}
                <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 ml-2 transition-transform duration-300 ${isExpanded ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/FeaturedCourses.tsx ---
import { useHomeStore } from '../../store/useHomeStore';
import { ChevronRight, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuthStore } from '../../store/useAuthStore';
import { useNavigationStore } from '../../store/useNavigationStore';
import { useImagensStore } from '../../store/useImagensStore';
import { getVagaImageFallback } from '../../lib/vagasImages';
import { optimizeImageUrl, formatCargaHoraria, getInstitutionLogo } from '../../lib/formatters';

export function FeaturedCourses() {
  const { user, setIsLoginOpen } = useAuthStore();
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();
  const { cursosDestaque: destaques, loading, instituicoes } = useHomeStore();
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();

  const handleNavigateCursos = () => {
    if (user) {
      setActiveTab('cursos');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      setIsLoginOpen(true);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <section className="bg-[#f9fafb] pt-16 md:pt-20 pb-12 min-h-[500px]">
        <div className="max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px]">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-6">Encontre seu curso</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
            {[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-[16px] h-96 border border-gray-100 p-4 flex flex-col justify-between animate-pulse">
                <div className="h-44 bg-gray-200 rounded-[12px]" />
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/4" />
                  <div className="h-6 bg-gray-200 rounded w-3/4" />
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-full" />
                </div>
                <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/2 mt-4" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (destaques.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="bg-[#f9fafb] pt-16 md:pt-20 pb-12 min-h-[500px]">
      <div className="max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px]">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-6">Encontre seu curso</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
          {destaques.map((curso, index) => {
            const imageUrl = curso.image || getVagaImageFallback(curso.id, imagensPublicas);
            const logoParceiro = getInstitutionLogo(instituicoes, curso.instituicaoCooperada);
            const titulo = curso.titulo || curso.tituloEditado || 'Curso sem título';
            const area = curso.nomeCineRotulo || curso.area || 'Conhecimento';
            const apresentacao = curso.apresentacao || curso.objetivo || 'Descrição não disponível para este curso. Inscreva-se para descobrir mais detalhes sobre a trilha de conhecimento.';
            
            return (
              <button 
                type="button"
                key={curso.id || index}
                className="flex flex-col text-left w-full h-full bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] overflow-hidden shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-md cursor-pointer group hc-white"
                onClick={handleNavigateCursos}
              >
                <div className="relative w-full aspect-video shrink-0 bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden min-h-0">
                  <img 
                    src={optimizeImageUrl(imageUrl, 400, 260)} 
                    alt=""
                    width="400" height="225" loading="lazy"
                    decoding="async"
                    className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col flex-grow p-[20px]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px] mb-[12px]">
                    <div className="w-[14px] h-[14px] rounded-full border-2 border-[#2563eb] bg-transparent shrink-0" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#374151] truncate">
                      {area}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#111827] line-clamp-2 min-h-[54px]">
                    {titulo}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-2 mb-4 text-sm text-[#374151] line-clamp-3">
                    {apresentacao}
                  </p>
                  
                  <div className="pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-row-reverse justify-between items-center mt-auto">
                    {logoParceiro ? (
                      <img 
                        src={logoParceiro} 
                        alt={curso.instituicaoCooperada} 
                        width="100"
                        height="28"
                        decoding="async"
                        className="hc-invert"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 truncate max-w-[60%]">
                        {curso.instituicaoCooperada || 'Instituição participante'}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    
                    {curso.cargaHoraria && (
                      <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-gray-500 gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span className="font-medium">{formatCargaHoraria(curso.cargaHoraria)}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-center mt-8">
          <button 
            onClick={handleNavigateCursos}
            className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[rgba(239,246,255,0.5)] border border-[#dbeafe] py-[10px] px-6 text-sm font-medium text-[#1e40af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#eff6ff] hc-border hc-no-underline"
          >
            Ver mais cursos
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-2" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/FeaturedVagas.tsx ---
import { useHomeStore } from '../../store/useHomeStore';
import { ChevronRight, MapPin, Clock, DollarSign } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuthStore } from '../../store/useAuthStore';
import { useNavigationStore } from '../../store/useNavigationStore';
import { useImagensStore } from '../../store/useImagensStore';
import { getVagaImageFallback, getVagaColorFallback } from '../../lib/vagasImages';
import { formatTime, formatCurrency, parseMonetaryValue, optimizeImageUrl, getInstitutionLogo, mapNivelEscolar } from '../../lib/formatters';

const parseBolsa = (valor: any) => parseMonetaryValue(valor);


export function FeaturedVagas() {
  const { user, setIsLoginOpen } = useAuthStore();
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();
  const { vagasDestaque: destaques, loading, instituicoes } = useHomeStore();
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();

  const handleNavigateVagas = () => {
    if (user) {
      setActiveTab('vagas');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      setIsLoginOpen(true);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <section className="bg-white pt-16 md:pt-20 pb-12 min-h-[500px]">
        <div className="max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px]">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-6">Conquiste sua vaga</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
            {[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-[16px] h-96 border border-gray-100 p-4 flex flex-col justify-between animate-pulse">
                <div className="h-44 bg-gray-200 rounded-[12px]" />
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/4" />
                  <div className="h-6 bg-gray-200 rounded w-3/4" />
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-full" />
                </div>
                <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/2 mt-4" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (destaques.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="bg-white pt-16 md:pt-20 pb-12 min-h-[500px]">
      <div className="max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px]">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-6">Conquiste sua vaga</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
          {destaques.map((vaga, index) => {
            const logoParceiro = getInstitutionLogo(instituicoes, vaga.instituicaoCooperada) || getInstitutionLogo(instituicoes, vaga.nomeDaEmpresa);

            return (
              <button 
                type="button"
                key={vaga.id || index}
                className="flex flex-col text-left w-full h-full bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] overflow-hidden shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-md cursor-pointer group hc-white"
                onClick={handleNavigateVagas}
              >
                <div 
                  className="relative w-full h-48 overflow-hidden flex shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: getVagaColorFallback(vaga.codigoDaVaga || vaga.id) }}
                >
                  <div className="absolute -top-10 -left-10 w-32 h-32 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
                  <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-[30%] w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-4 border-white/10" />
                  
                  <div className="relative z-10 w-[55%] p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[8px]">
                    <span className="text-white font-bold text-xl leading-snug drop-shadow-md capitalize mb-2">
                      {vaga.tipoDeVaga ? vaga.tipoDeVaga.toLowerCase() : 'Oportunidade'}
                    </span>
                    {vaga.cidade && (
                      <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-xs font-medium gap-1.5">
                        <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate">{vaga.cidade}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {vaga.horarioEntrada && vaga.horarioSaida && (
                      <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-xs font-medium gap-1.5">
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate">
                          {formatTime(vaga.horarioEntrada)} às {formatTime(vaga.horarioSaida)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  {((vaga.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTAGIO') || vaga.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTÁGIO')) && parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0) ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-xs font-medium gap-1.5">
                      <DollarSign className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata))} {vaga.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vaga.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ) : ((vaga.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('APRENDIZ')) && parseBolsa(vaga.salarioExato) > 0) ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-xs font-medium gap-1.5">
                      <DollarSign className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vaga.salarioExato))} {vaga.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vaga.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ) : ((parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 || parseBolsa(vaga.salarioExato) > 0)) && (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-xs font-medium gap-1.5">
                      <DollarSign className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 ? formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata)) : formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vaga.salarioExato))} {vaga.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vaga.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : vaga.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vaga.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 w-[45%] z-10">
                    <img 
                      src={optimizeImageUrl(vaga.image || getVagaImageFallback(vaga.codigoDaVaga || vaga.id, imagensPublicas), 400, 260)} 
                      alt=""
                      width="400" height="225" loading="lazy"
                      decoding="async"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex flex-col flex-grow p-[20px]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px] mb-[12px]">
                    <div className="w-[14px] h-[14px] rounded-full border-2 border-[#2563eb] bg-transparent shrink-0" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#374151] truncate">
                      {vaga.areaProfissional || vaga.areaDeAtuacao || 'Área não informada'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#111827] line-clamp-2">
                      {vaga.nomeDaEmpresa || 'Empresa Confidencial'}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-2 mb-4 text-sm text-[#374151] line-clamp-3">
                    {vaga.atividades || 'Descrição não disponível para esta vaga. Inscreva-se para descobrir mais detalhes sobre a oportunidade.'}
                  </p>
                  
                  <div className="pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-row-reverse justify-between items-center mt-auto">
                    {logoParceiro ? (
                      <img 
                        src={logoParceiro} 
                        alt={vaga.instituicaoCooperada || vaga.nomeDaEmpresa} 
                        className="h-7 max-w-[70%] object-contain object-right hc-invert"
                        width="100" height="28" loading="lazy"
                        decoding="async"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 truncate max-w-[60%]">
                        {vaga.instituicaoCooperada || vaga.nomeDaEmpresa || 'Instituição participante'}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    
                    {vaga.nivelEscolar && (
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded-md shrink-0">
                        {mapNivelEscolar(vaga.nivelEscolar)}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex justify-center mt-8">
          <button 
            onClick={handleNavigateVagas}
            className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[rgba(239,246,255,0.5)] border border-[#dbeafe] py-[10px] px-6 text-sm font-medium text-[#1e40af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#eff6ff] hc-border hc-no-underline"
          >
            Ver mais vagas
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-2" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import { useAuthStore } from '../../store/useAuthStore';

const STEPS = [
  {
    id: 1,
    icon: UserCheck,
    title: "Faça seu Cadastro",
    description: "Faça seu cadastro gratuito em um minuto para desbloquear o acesso total e ilimitado ao catálogo de cursos e vagas."
  },
  {
    id: 2,
    icon: BookOpen,
    title: "Escolha seus Cursos ou Vagas",

    id: 3,
    icon: CheckCircle2,
    title: "Registre suas Conquistas",
    description: "Concluiu um curso ou garantiu uma vaga? Retorne à plataforma para enviar seu certificado ou informar sua contratação."
  }
];

export function HowItWorks() {
  const { user, setIsLoginOpen } = useAuthStore();
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();

  const handleCtaClick = () => {
    if (user) {
      setActiveTab('inicio');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      setIsLoginOpen(true);
    }
  };
  
  return (
    <section  className="w-full bg-[#f9fafb] py-16 md:py-20">
      <div 
        className="max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px]"
      >
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-left mb-10">
          Entenda como funciona
        </h2>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-[24px]">
          {STEPS.map((step) => {
            const Icon = step.icon;
            return (
              <div 
                key={step.id}
                className="relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col min-h-[16.25rem] rounded-[16px] p-8 bg-white border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm hover:border-[#004b87]/40 hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300 group"
              >
                <div 
                  className={`absolute -bottom-4 z-0 pointer-events-none select-none text-[192px] leading-none font-black text-slate-100 group-hover:text-blue-50 transition-colors duration-300 hc-black ${
                    step.id === 1 ? '-right-10 md:-right-4' : '-right-4'
                  }`}
                >
                  {step.id}
                </div>
                
                <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 flex items-center justify-center mb-6">
                    <Icon className="w-6 h-6 text-[#004b87]" />
                  </div>
                  
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">
                    {step.title}
                  </h3>
                  
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-[1.625]">
                    {step.description}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex justify-center mt-12 relative z-10">
          <button 
            onClick={handleCtaClick}
            className="group inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] rounded-full bg-blue-900 py-[12px] px-8 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-all duration-150 hover:bg-blue-950 hover:shadow-lg hc-border hc-no-underline"

}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/Institutions.tsx ---
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useHomeStore } from '../../store/useHomeStore';
import { useInView } from '../../hooks/useInView';
import { optimizeImageUrl } from '../../lib/formatters';

function InstitutionLogo({ name, logoUrl, linkUrl }: { name: string; logoUrl?: string; linkUrl?: string }) {
  const [imgError, setImgError] = useState(false);

  const formatExternalUrl = (url?: string): string | undefined => {
    if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return undefined;
    try {
      const trimmed = url.trim();
      if (!trimmed.startsWith('https://')) return undefined;
      const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
      return parsed.protocol === 'https:' && parsed.hostname ? parsed.href : undefined;
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    }
  };

  const formattedUrl = formatExternalUrl(linkUrl);

  const logoContent = (
    <>
      {logoUrl && !imgError ? (
        <img
          src={optimizeImageUrl(logoUrl, 260)}
          alt={`Logo ${name}`}
          width="200" height="106" loading="lazy"
          decoding="async"
          className="w-auto max-w-full h-[76px] sm:h-[90px] md:h-[106px] object-contain transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105 hc-invert"
          style={{ imageRendering: '-webkit-optimize-contrast' }}
          onError={() => setImgError(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <span className="text-lg md:text-xl font-[800] text-[#003865] tracking-tight leading-[1.25] text-center transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105">
          {name}
        </span>
      )}
    </>
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center group">
      {formattedUrl ? (
        <a href={formattedUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center outline-none">
          {logoContent}
        </a>
      ) : (
        <div className="cursor-default flex items-center justify-center outline-none">
          {logoContent}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Institutions() {
  const { instituicoes, loading } = useHomeStore();
  const ref = useRef(null);
  useInView(ref, { once: true, margin: "300px 0px" });

  if (instituicoes.length === 0 && !loading) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <section ref={ref}  className="w-full bg-[#ffffff] py-16 md:py-24 min-h-[300px]">
      <div 
        className="max-w-[896px] mx-auto px-[16px]"
      >
        <div className="max-w-[48rem] mx-auto text-center mb-16">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] mb-4">
            Instituições Participantes
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#4b5563] leading-[1.625]">
            O CRA-SP Educa é construído em parceria com instituições de excelência, comprometidas com<br className="hidden md:block" /> o desenvolvimento acadêmico, a evolução profissional e a inserção no mercado de trabalho.
          </p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="w-full">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 items-center justify-items-center gap-x-12 gap-y-6 sm:gap-x-16 sm:gap-y-8 md:gap-x-20 md:gap-y-10">
            {instituicoes.map((inst, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center scale-[0.80] sm:scale-[0.85] md:scale-[0.90]">
                <InstitutionLogo 
                  name={inst.sigla || inst.instituicao || inst.instituicaoCooperada || 'Instituição'} 
                  logoUrl={inst.logoUrl}
                  linkUrl={inst.site}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/Institutions.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/JornadaCursoCard.tsx ---
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ConfirmationModal } from '../ui/ConfirmationModal';
import { sanitizeUrlForOpenRedirect } from '../../lib/validators';
import { useImagensStore } from '../../store/useImagensStore';
import { getVagaImageFallback } from '../../lib/vagasImages';
import { optimizeImageUrl } from '../../lib/formatters';
import { useCursosStore } from '../../store/useCursosStore';

type JornadaCursoCardProps = Readonly<{
  jornada: any;
  cursoSnapshot: any;
  onUpdateStatus: (id: string, status: string) => void;
  onEvaluate: (jornada: any) => void;
}>;

export function JornadaCursoCard({ jornada, cursoSnapshot, onUpdateStatus, onEvaluate }: JornadaCursoCardProps) {
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();
  const cursos = useCursosStore((s) => s.cursos);
  const cursoNoCatalogo = cursos.find((c) => c.id === (jornada.cursoId || jornada.id));

  const [modalConfig, setModalConfig] = useState<{
    isOpen: boolean;
    variant: 'danger' | 'success';
    title: string;
    description: string;
    cancelText: string;
    confirmText: string;
    onConfirm: () => void;
  } | null>(null);

  const titulo = cursoSnapshot?.titulo || cursoSnapshot?.tituloEditado || cursoNoCatalogo?.titulo || cursoNoCatalogo?.tituloEditado || 'Curso Indisponível';
  const instituicao = cursoSnapshot?.instituicaoCooperada || cursoNoCatalogo?.instituicaoCooperada || 'Instituição Desconhecida';
  const linkDoCurso = cursoSnapshot?.linkDoCurso || cursoSnapshot?.link || cursoSnapshot?.url || cursoSnapshot?.site || cursoSnapshot?.linkDeInscricao || cursoNoCatalogo?.linkDoCurso || cursoNoCatalogo?.link;
  
  const dataPreMatricula = jornada.createdAt ? new Date(jornada.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') : null;
  const dataMatricula = jornada.matriculadoAt ? new Date(jornada.matriculadoAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') : null;

  const labelData = jornada.status === 'PRE_MATRICULADO'
    ? (dataPreMatricula ? `Pré-matrícula em: ${dataPreMatricula}` : "")
    : (dataMatricula ? `Matriculado em: ${dataMatricula}` : (dataPreMatricula ? `Adicionado em: ${dataPreMatricula}` : ""));

  let cardOpacity = "opacity-100";
  let tagBg = "bg-gray-100";
  let tagText = "text-gray-800";
  let tagDot = "bg-gray-400";
  let tagLabel = jornada.status;

  if (jornada.status === 'PRE_MATRICULADO') {
    tagBg = "bg-blue-50";
    tagText = "text-blue-700";
    tagDot = "bg-blue-500";
    tagLabel = "Pré-Matriculado";
  } else if (jornada.status === 'MATRICULADO' || jornada.status === 'AGUARDANDO_CERTIFICADO') {
    tagBg = "bg-amber-50";
    tagText = "text-amber-700";
    tagDot = "bg-amber-500 animate-pulse";
    tagLabel = "Em Andamento";
  } else if (jornada.status === 'EM_ANALISE') {
    cardOpacity = "opacity-80";
    tagBg = "bg-teal-50";
    tagText = "text-teal-700";
    tagDot = "bg-teal-500";
    tagLabel = "Em Análise";
  } else if (jornada.status === 'CONCLUIDO') {
    tagBg = "bg-emerald-50";
    tagText = "text-emerald-700";
    tagDot = "bg-emerald-500";
    tagLabel = "Concluído";
  }

  const handleDesistir = () => {
    setModalConfig({
      isOpen: true,
      variant: 'danger',
      title: 'Remover da lista?',
      description: 'O curso será removido da sua lista de andamento e arquivado.',
      cancelText: 'Cancelar',
      confirmText: 'Sim, remover',
      onConfirm: () => {
        onUpdateStatus(jornada.id, 'DESISTIU');
        setModalConfig(null);
      },
    });
  };

  const handleMatriculado = () => {
    onUpdateStatus(jornada.id, 'MATRICULADO');
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className={`bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col h-full ${cardOpacity}`}>
        <div className="w-full aspect-video relative shrink-0 bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden min-h-0">
          <img 
            src={optimizeImageUrl(cursoSnapshot?.image || cursoNoCatalogo?.image || getVagaImageFallback(cursoSnapshot?.codigoDaVaga || jornada.cursoId, imagensPublicas), 400, 260)} 
            alt={titulo} 
            width="400" height="225" loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
            className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover" 
            referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="p-[20px] flex flex-col flex-grow">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
            <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${tagBg} ${tagText}`}>
              <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${tagDot}`}></span>
              {tagLabel}
            </div>
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-tight mb-2 truncate" title={titulo}>{titulo}</h3>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[#6b7280] line-clamp-1 font-medium mb-1" title={instituicao}>{instituicao}</p>
          {labelData && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium mb-4">{labelData}</p>
          )}

          <div className="mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col gap-2">
            {linkDoCurso && (jornada.status === 'PRE_MATRICULADO' || jornada.status === 'MATRICULADO' || jornada.status === 'AGUARDANDO_CERTIFICADO') && (
              <a 
                href={sanitizeUrlForOpenRedirect(linkDoCurso)} 
                target="_blank" 
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
              >
                Acessar AVA da Instituição
              </a>
            )}

            {jornada.status === 'PRE_MATRICULADO' && (
              <>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleMatriculado}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white hover:bg-[#1e40af] text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Já iniciei o curso
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleDesistir}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Remover da lista
                </button>
              </>
            )}

            {(jornada.status === 'MATRICULADO' || jornada.status === 'AGUARDANDO_CERTIFICADO') && (
              <>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => onEvaluate(jornada)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-700 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Enviar Certificado
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleDesistir}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Não vou mais cursar
                </button>
              </>
            )}

            {jornada.status === 'EM_ANALISE' && (
              <div className="w-full text-center px-4 py-2.5 bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm">
                Certificado em Análise
              </div>
            )}

            {jornada.status === 'CONCLUIDO' && (
              <div className="w-full text-center px-4 py-2.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm">
                Concluído e Aprovado
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {modalConfig && (
        <ConfirmationModal
          isOpen={modalConfig.isOpen}
          variant={modalConfig.variant}
          title={modalConfig.title}
          description={modalConfig.description}
          cancelText={modalConfig.cancelText}
          confirmText={modalConfig.confirmText}
          onCancel={() => setModalConfig(null)}
          onConfirm={modalConfig.onConfirm}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/JornadaCursosKpis.tsx ---
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { JornadaCurso } from '../../store/useJornadaCursosStore';
import { Clock, BookOpen, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react';

interface JornadaCursosKpisProps {
  jornadaCursos: readonly JornadaCurso[];
}

export function JornadaCursosKpis({ jornadaCursos }: JornadaCursosKpisProps) {
  const kpis = useMemo(() => {
    let preMatriculados = 0;
    let matriculados = 0;
    let concluidos = 0;
    let horasCertificadas = 0;

    jornadaCursos.forEach((j) => {
      if (j.status === "PRE_MATRICULADO") preMatriculados++;
      if (j.status === "MATRICULADO" || (j.status as any) === "CURSANDO") matriculados++;
      if (j.status === "CONCLUIDO") {
        concluidos++;
        const cargaHoraria = (j as any).cursoSnapshot?.cargaHoraria;
        if (cargaHoraria) {
          const match = String(cargaHoraria).match(/\d+/);
          if (match) horasCertificadas += parseInt(match[0], 10);
        }
      }
    });

    return { preMatriculados, matriculados, concluidos, horasCertificadas };
  }, [jornadaCursos]);

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

        <div className="bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[12px]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px]">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#fefce8] text-[#ca8a04]">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#6b7280]">
              Cursos em<br />Pré-matrícula
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
            {kpis.preMatriculados}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[12px]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px]">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#eff6ff] text-[#2563eb]">
              <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#6b7280]">
              Cursos em<br />Andamento
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
            {kpis.matriculados}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[12px]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px]">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#f0fdf4] text-[#16a34a]">
              <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#6b7280]">
              Cursos<br />Concluídos
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
            {kpis.concluidos}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#ffffff] border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] shadow-sm p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[12px]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-[8px]">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#ecfeff] text-[#0891b2]">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#6b7280]">
              Horas de<br />Estudo
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
            {kpis.horasCertificadas}h
          </span>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/JornadaVagaCard.tsx ---
import { useState } from 'react';
import { MapPin, Banknote, Landmark } from 'lucide-react';
import { ConfirmationModal } from '../ui/ConfirmationModal';
import { sanitizeUrlForOpenRedirect } from '../../lib/validators';
import { getVagaImageFallback } from '../../lib/vagasImages';
import { useImagensStore } from '../../store/useImagensStore';
import { useVagasStore } from '../../store/useVagasStore';
import { formatCurrency, parseMonetaryValue } from '../../lib/formatters';
type JornadaVagaCardProps = Readonly<{
  jornadaVaga: any;
  vagaSnapshot: any;
  onUpdateStatus: (id: string, status: string) => void;
  index?: number;
}>;
const FIXED_COLORS = [
  '#004e9a',
  '#0369a1',
  '#0f766e',
  '#1d4ed8'
];
export function JornadaVagaCard({ jornadaVaga, vagaSnapshot, onUpdateStatus, index = 0 }: JornadaVagaCardProps) {
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();
  const vagas = useVagasStore(s => s.vagas);
  const vagaOriginal = vagas.find(v => (v.id || v.codigoDaVaga) === (vagaSnapshot?.codigoDaVaga || jornadaVaga.vagaId));
  const [modalConfig, setModalConfig] = useState<{
    isOpen: boolean;
    variant: 'danger' | 'success';
    title: string;
    description: string;
    cancelText: string;
    confirmText: string;
    onConfirm: () => void;
  } | null>(null);
  const cargo = vagaSnapshot?.tipoDeVaga || vagaOriginal?.tipoDeVaga || 'Oportunidade';
  const empresa = vagaSnapshot?.nomeDaEmpresa1 || vagaSnapshot?.nomeDaEmpresa || vagaOriginal?.nomeDaEmpresa1 || vagaOriginal?.nomeDaEmpresa || 'Empresa Confidencial';
  const localidade = vagaSnapshot?.cidade || vagaOriginal?.cidade || 'Local não informado';
  const instituicao = vagaSnapshot?.instituicaoCooperada || vagaOriginal?.instituicaoCooperada;
  const rawBolsa = parseMonetaryValue(vagaSnapshot?.bolsaAuxilioExata || vagaOriginal?.bolsaAuxilioExata);
  const rawSalario = parseMonetaryValue(vagaSnapshot?.salarioExato || vagaOriginal?.salarioExato);
  const hasBolsa = rawBolsa > 0;
  const hasSalario = rawSalario > 0;
  const tipoAuxilio = vagaSnapshot?.tipoAuxilioBolsa || vagaOriginal?.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vagaSnapshot?.tipoAuxilioBolsa || vagaOriginal?.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : '';
  const linkDeInscricao = vagaSnapshot?.linkDeInscricao || vagaOriginal?.linkDeInscricao || vagaSnapshot?.link || vagaOriginal?.link || vagaSnapshot?.url || vagaOriginal?.url || vagaSnapshot?.site || vagaOriginal?.site;
  const imageUrl = vagaSnapshot?.imageUrl || vagaSnapshot?.image || vagaOriginal?.image || vagaOriginal?.imageUrl;
  const dataPreCandidatura = jornadaVaga.createdAt ? new Date(jornadaVaga.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') : null;
  const dataCandidatura = jornadaVaga.candidatadoAt ? new Date(jornadaVaga.candidatadoAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') : null;
  const labelData = jornadaVaga.status === 'PRE_CANDIDATADO'
    ? (dataPreCandidatura ? `Pré-participação em: ${dataPreCandidatura}` : "")
    : (dataCandidatura ? `Participando desde: ${dataCandidatura}` : (dataPreCandidatura ? `Adicionado em: ${dataPreCandidatura}` : ""));
  let tagBg = "bg-gray-100";
  let tagText = "text-gray-800";
  let tagDot = "bg-gray-400";
  let tagLabel = jornadaVaga.status;
  let actionOpacity = "opacity-100";
  const status = jornadaVaga.status;
  if (status === 'PRE_CANDIDATADO') {
    tagBg = "bg-blue-50";
    tagText = "text-blue-700";
    tagDot = "bg-blue-500";
    tagLabel = "Interesse";
  } else if (status === 'CANDIDATADO') {
    tagBg = "bg-amber-50";
    tagText = "text-amber-700";
    tagDot = "bg-amber-500 animate-pulse";
    tagLabel = "Em andamento";
  } else if (status === 'CONTRATADO') {
    tagBg = "bg-emerald-50";
    tagText = "text-emerald-700";
    tagDot = "bg-emerald-500";
    tagLabel = "Contratado";
  } else if (status === 'NAO_FOI_DESTA_VEZ' || status === 'DESISTIU') {
    actionOpacity = "opacity-75";
    tagBg = "bg-gray-100";
    tagText = "text-gray-800";
    tagDot = "bg-gray-500";
    tagLabel = "Encerrado";
  }
  const handleDesistir = () => {
    setModalConfig({
      isOpen: true,
      variant: 'danger',
      title: 'Remover da lista?',
      description: 'A vaga será removida da sua lista de andamento e arquivada.',
      cancelText: 'Cancelar',
      confirmText: 'Sim, remover',
      onConfirm: () => {
        onUpdateStatus(jornadaVaga.id, 'DESISTIU');
        setModalConfig(null);
      },
    });
  };
  const handleCandidatado = () => {
    onUpdateStatus(jornadaVaga.id, 'CANDIDATADO');
  };
  const handleNaoFoiDestaVez = () => {
    setModalConfig({
      isOpen: true,
      variant: 'danger',
      title: 'Encerrar processo?',
      description: 'Deseja marcar este processo como "Não foi desta vez"? Ele será movido para o histórico.',
      cancelText: 'Cancelar',
      confirmText: 'Sim, encerrar',
      onConfirm: () => {
        onUpdateStatus(jornadaVaga.id, 'NAO_FOI_DESTA_VEZ');
        setModalConfig(null);
      },
    });
  };
  const cardColor = FIXED_COLORS[index % FIXED_COLORS.length];
  return (
    <>
      <div className={`bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col h-full ${actionOpacity}`}>
        <div 
          className="relative w-full aspect-video overflow-hidden flex shrink-0 min-h-0"
          style={{ backgroundColor: cardColor }}
        >
          <div className="absolute -top-10 -left-10 w-32 h-32 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-[30%] w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-4 border-white/10" />
          <div className="relative z-10 w-[55%] p-[20px] flex flex-col justify-center gap-[8px]">
            <span className="text-white font-bold text-xl leading-snug drop-shadow-md capitalize mb-2 line-clamp-3">
              {cargo.toLowerCase()}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 w-[45%] z-10">
            <img 
              src={imageUrl || getVagaImageFallback(vagaSnapshot?.codigoDaVaga || jornadaVaga.vagaId, imagensPublicas)}
              alt={cargo}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover object-center"
              width="400" height="225" loading="lazy"
              referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-5 flex flex-col flex-grow">
          <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
            <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${tagBg} ${tagText}`}>
              <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${tagDot}`}></span>
              {tagLabel}
            </div>
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-[#111827] leading-tight mb-2 truncate" title={empresa}>{empresa}</h3>
          {labelData && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium mb-3">{labelData}</p>
          )}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 text-[13px] text-gray-600 mb-4 font-medium mt-auto">
            {instituicao && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Landmark className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-gray-400" />
                <span className="line-clamp-1" title={instituicao}>{instituicao}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-gray-400" />
              <span className="line-clamp-1" title={localidade}>{localidade}</span>
            </div>
            {(hasBolsa || hasSalario) && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Banknote className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-green-600" />
                <span className="line-clamp-1 font-semibold text-green-700">
                  R$ {hasBolsa ? formatCurrency(rawBolsa) : formatCurrency(rawSalario)} {tipoAuxilio}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-col gap-2">
            {status === 'PRE_CANDIDATADO' && (
              <>
                {linkDeInscricao && (
                  <a 
                    href={sanitizeUrlForOpenRedirect(linkDeInscricao)} 
                    target="_blank" 
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                  >
                    Acessar Portal da Vaga
                  </a>
                )}
                <button 
                  onClick={handleCandidatado}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white hover:bg-[#1e40af] text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Já estou participando
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleDesistir}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Remover da lista
                </button>
              </>
            )}
            {status === 'CANDIDATADO' && (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => onUpdateStatus(jornadaVaga.id, 'CONTRATADO')}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#1e3a8a] hover:bg-[#1e40af] text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors shadow-sm text-center"
                >
                  Fui Contratado!
                </button>
                <button 
                  onClick={handleNaoFoiDestaVez}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors text-center"
                >
                  Não foi desta vez
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
            {status === 'CONTRATADO' && (
              <div className="w-full text-center px-4 py-2.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm">
                Processo Aprovado!
              </div>
            )}
            {(status === 'NAO_FOI_DESTA_VEZ' || status === 'DESISTIU') && (
              <div className="w-full text-center px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm">
                Processo Encerrado
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {modalConfig && (
        <ConfirmationModal
          isOpen={modalConfig.isOpen}
          variant={modalConfig.variant}
          title={modalConfig.title}
          description={modalConfig.description}
          cancelText={modalConfig.cancelText}
          confirmText={modalConfig.confirmText}
          onCancel={() => setModalConfig(null)}
          onConfirm={modalConfig.onConfirm}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/ListaJornadaCursos.tsx ---
import { JornadaCursoCard } from "./JornadaCursoCard";
import { ChevronRight, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigationStore } from "../../store/useNavigationStore";

export type ListaJornadaCursosProps = Readonly<{
  jornadaCursos: any[];
  handleUpdateStatus: (id: string, newStatus: string) => Promise<void>;
  setEvaluatingJornada: (jornada: any) => void;
}>;

export function ListaJornadaCursos({
  jornadaCursos,
  handleUpdateStatus,
  setEvaluatingJornada,
}: ListaJornadaCursosProps) {
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();
  const jornadasAtivas = [...jornadaCursos]
    .filter((j) => j.status !== "DESISTIU" && j.status !== "CONCLUIDO")
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const tituloA = (a.cursoSnapshot?.titulo || a.cursoSnapshot?.tituloEditado || "").toLowerCase();
      const tituloB = (b.cursoSnapshot?.titulo || b.cursoSnapshot?.tituloEditado || "").toLowerCase();
      return tituloA.localeCompare(tituloB);
    });

  return (
    <section>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e5e7eb] pb-2 mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
          Meus Cursos
        </h2>
        <button 
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('cursos')}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-medium text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"
        >
          Explorar cursos
          <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {jornadasAtivas.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 border-dashed rounded-[16px] py-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <BookOpen className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-400 mb-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <h3 className="text-base font-medium text-gray-500">
            Nenhum curso em andamento
          </h3>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          {jornadasAtivas.map((jornada) => (
            <JornadaCursoCard
              key={jornada.id}
              jornada={jornada}
              cursoSnapshot={jornada.cursoSnapshot || {}}
              onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
              onEvaluate={setEvaluatingJornada}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { JornadaVagaCard } from "./JornadaVagaCard";
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigationStore } from "../../store/useNavigationStore";

export type ListaJornadaVagasProps = Readonly<{
  jornadaVagas: any[];
  handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus: (id: string, newStatus: string) => Promise<void>;
}>;

export function ListaJornadaVagas({
  jornadaVagas,
  handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus,
}: ListaJornadaVagasProps) {
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();
  const jornadaVagasVisiveis = [...jornadaVagas]
    .filter((j) => !['NAO_FOI_DESTA_VEZ', 'CONTRATADO', 'DESISTIU'].includes(j.status))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const empresaA = (a.vagaSnapshot?.nomeDaEmpresa || "").toLowerCase();
      const empresaB = (b.vagaSnapshot?.nomeDaEmpresa || "").toLowerCase();
      return empresaA.localeCompare(empresaB);
    });

  if (jornadaVagasVisiveis.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <section>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e5e7eb] pb-2 mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
          Minhas Vagas
        </h2>
        <button 
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('vagas')}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-medium text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"
        >
          Explorar vagas
          <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        {jornadaVagasVisiveis.map((jornadaVaga, index) => (
          <JornadaVagaCard
            key={jornadaVaga.id}
            jornadaVaga={jornadaVaga}
            vagaSnapshot={jornadaVaga.vagaSnapshot || {}}
            onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus}
            index={index}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/showcase/SocialProof.tsx ---
import { ChevronRight, FileCheck, Users, MonitorPlay, Briefcase } from 'lucide-react';
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useAuthStore } from '../../store/useAuthStore';
import { useNavigationStore } from '../../store/useNavigationStore';
import { useHomeStore } from '../../store/useHomeStore';
import { useInView } from '../../hooks/useInView';

function CountUp({ end, duration = 2000, startAnimation = true }: { end: number; duration?: number; startAnimation?: boolean }) {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let animationFrame: number;
    let startTime: number;

    const update = (timestamp: number) => {
      if (!startTime) startTime = timestamp;
      const progress = timestamp - startTime;
      const percentage = Math.min(progress / duration, 1);
      
      const easeOut = (t: number) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      setCount(Math.floor(end * easeOut(percentage)));
      
      if (progress < duration) {
        animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(update);
      }
    };

    if (startAnimation && end > 0) {
      animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(update);
    }

    return () => {
      if (animationFrame) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrame);
      }
    };
  }, [end, duration, startAnimation]);

  return <>{count.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</>;
}

export function SocialProof() {
  const { user, setIsLoginOpen, totalUsuarios, totalMatriculas, fetchTotais } = useAuthStore();
  const { setActiveTab } = useNavigationStore();
  const { stats } = useHomeStore();

  const handleCtaClick = () => {
    if (user) {
      setActiveTab('inicio');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      setIsLoginOpen(true);
    }
  };
  
  const totalDeCursos = stats?.totalDeCursos || 0;
  const totalDeVagas = stats?.totalDeVagas || 0;

  const ref = useRef(null);
  const isInView = useInView(ref, { once: true, margin: "300px 0px" });

  const visibilityRef = useRef(null);
  const isVisible = useInView(visibilityRef, { once: true, margin: "-50px" });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isInView) {
      fetchTotais();
    }
  }, [fetchTotais, isInView]);

  const metrics = useMemo(() => [
    { 
      id: 1, 
      label: 'matrículas realizadas', 
      value: totalMatriculas ?? 0,
      icon: FileCheck
    },
    { 
      id: 2, 
      label: 'usuários cadastrados', 
      value: totalUsuarios ?? 0,
      icon: Users
    },
    { 
      id: 3, 
      label: 'cursos gratuitos', 
      value: totalDeCursos,
      icon: MonitorPlay
    },
    { 
      id: 4, 
      label: 'oportunidades de mercado', 
      value: totalDeVagas,
      icon: Briefcase
    }
  ], [totalMatriculas, totalUsuarios, totalDeCursos, totalDeVagas]);

  return (
    <section ref={ref} className="relative overflow-hidden bg-[#172554] py-16 md:py-24 min-h-[450px]">
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/4 w-[800px] h-[800px] bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_center,rgba(59,130,246,0.15)_0%,transparent_70%)] rounded-full pointer-events-none -translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/2" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-1/4 w-[600px] h-[600px] bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_center,rgba(6,182,212,0.15)_0%,transparent_70%)] rounded-full pointer-events-none translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2" />
      
      <div className="relative z-10 max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-6">
        <div className="max-w-[48rem] mx-auto mb-16 text-center">
          <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl lg:text-5xl font-bold text-white mb-4 tracking-tight">
            Dê o seu <span className="text-[#eab308]">próximo passo</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-base lg:text-lg text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">
            Junte-se a milhares de estudantes e acelere a sua jornada na Administração.
          </p>
        </div>
        
        <div ref={visibilityRef} className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-y-12 md:gap-y-16 lg:gap-y-0 pt-8 relative">
          {metrics.map((metric, index) => (
            <div 
              key={metric.id}
              className={`flex flex-col items-center text-center group ${
                index !== metrics.length - 1 ? 'lg:border-r border-white/[0.08]' : ''
              }`}
            >
              <metric.icon 
                className="w-8 h-8 md:w-10 md:h-10 text-[#38bdf8] mb-4 md:mb-5 transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:-translate-y-1 group-hover:scale-110" 
                strokeWidth={1.5} 
              />

              <div className="flex flex-col items-center px-4">
                <span className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold text-white mb-1 tracking-tight">
                  <CountUp end={metric.value} startAnimation={isVisible} />
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
                  {metric.label}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex justify-center mt-16 relative z-10">
          <button 
            onClick={handleCtaClick}
            className="group flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] rounded-full bg-[#eab308] py-[12px] px-8 text-sm font-bold text-black transition-all duration-300 hover:bg-[#facc15] hover:shadow-[0_10px_15px_-3px_rgba(234,179,8,0.2)] hc-border hc-no-underline"
          >
            Fazer parte do CRA-SP Educa
            <ChevronRight className="w-[18px] h-[18px] transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
