--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/SearchableSelect.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useId } from 'react';

interface Option {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

interface SearchableSelectProps {
  options: Option[];
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  placeholder: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  id?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}

export function SearchableSelect({ options, value, onChange, placeholder, disabled, id, ariaLabel }: SearchableSelectProps) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const optionRefs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);
  const generatedId = useId();
  const triggerId = id || `select-trigger-${generatedId}`;
  const listboxId = `select-listbox-${generatedId}`;
  const searchInputId = `select-search-${generatedId}`;

  const selectedOption = options.find(o => o.value === value);

  const closeDropdown = (restoreFocus: boolean) => {
    if (restoreFocus && triggerRef.current) {
      triggerRef.current.focus();
    }
    setIsOpen(false);
    if (restoreFocus) {
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        triggerRef.current?.focus();
      });
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      if (wrapperRef.current && !wrapperRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const filteredOptions = options.filter(o => 
    o.label.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveIndex(0);
  }, [searchTerm, isOpen]);

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      if (isOpen) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        closeDropdown(true);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!isOpen && (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp')) {
      e.preventDefault();
      setIsOpen(true);
      setSearchTerm('');
      return;
    }
    if (isOpen) {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (filteredOptions.length === 0) return;
        const focusedIdx = optionRefs.current.findIndex(el => el !== null && el === document.activeElement);
        const baseIdx = focusedIdx >= 0 ? focusedIdx : activeIndex;
        const nextIdx = (baseIdx + 1) % filteredOptions.length;
        setActiveIndex(nextIdx);
        if (focusedIdx >= 0) {
          optionRefs.current[nextIdx]?.focus();
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (filteredOptions.length === 0) return;
        const focusedIdx = optionRefs.current.findIndex(el => el !== null && el === document.activeElement);
        const baseIdx = focusedIdx >= 0 ? focusedIdx : activeIndex;
        const nextIdx = (baseIdx - 1 + filteredOptions.length) % filteredOptions.length;
        setActiveIndex(nextIdx);
        if (focusedIdx >= 0) {
          optionRefs.current[nextIdx]?.focus();
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        const focusedIdx = optionRefs.current.findIndex(el => el !== null && el === document.activeElement);
        const targetIdx = focusedIdx >= 0 ? focusedIdx : activeIndex;
        if (filteredOptions[targetIdx]) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          onChange(filteredOptions[targetIdx].value);
          closeDropdown(true);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full text-gray-900 font-normal" ref={wrapperRef} onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}>
      <button 
        ref={triggerRef}
        id={triggerId}
        type="button"
        role="combobox"
        aria-label={ariaLabel || placeholder}
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        aria-haspopup="listbox"
        aria-controls={listboxId}
        disabled={disabled}
        className={`w-full text-left border border-gray-300 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-gray-50 flex justify-between items-center transition-all text-sm ${disabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : 'cursor-pointer hover:bg-white'} ${isOpen ? 'border-blue-900 ring-1 ring-blue-900 bg-white' : ''}`}
        onClick={() => {
          if (!disabled) {
            setIsOpen(!isOpen);
            setSearchTerm('');
          }
        }}
      >
        <span className="truncate">{selectedOption ? selectedOption.label : placeholder}</span>
        <span className={`text-xs transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-blue-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`} aria-hidden="true">▼</span>
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1.5 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 flex flex-col overflow-hidden text-sm">
          <input
            ref={searchInputRef}
            id={searchInputId}
            name="searchable-select"
            type="text"
            aria-label={`Pesquisar ${ariaLabel || placeholder}`}
            aria-controls={listboxId}
            aria-activedescendant={filteredOptions[activeIndex] ? `${listboxId}-opt-${activeIndex}` : undefined}
            className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border-b border-gray-100 outline-none bg-gray-50 focus:bg-white transition-colors"
            placeholder="Pesquisar..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={e => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
          />
          <div id={listboxId} role="listbox" aria-label={ariaLabel || placeholder} className="overflow-y-auto">
            {filteredOptions.length > 0 ? (
              filteredOptions.map((option, idx) => {
                const isSelected = option.value === value;
                const isHighlighted = idx === activeIndex;
                return (
                  <button
                    ref={el => { optionRefs.current[idx] = el; }}
                    id={`${listboxId}-opt-${idx}`}
                    type="button"
                    role="option"
                    aria-selected={isSelected}
                    key={option.value}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3.5 py-2.5 cursor-pointer truncate transition-colors ${isHighlighted ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-900 font-medium' : 'hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-900'}`}
                    onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(idx)}
                    onFocus={() => setActiveIndex(idx)}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onChange(option.value);
                      closeDropdown(true);
                    }}
                  >
                    {option.label}
                  </button>
                );
              })
            ) : (
              <div className="px-[16px] py-[12px] text-gray-500 text-sm" role="status">Nenhum resultado</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/SearchableSelect.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/UploadCertificadoModal.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { compressImage } from '../../lib/compressor';
import { X, UploadCloud, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import { lockScroll, unlockScroll, useModalAccessibility } from '../../lib/modalScroll';
import { AuthSessionSnapshot, getAuthSessionSnapshot, isAuthSessionCurrent } from '../../store/useAuthStore';

type UploadCertificadoModalProps = Readonly<{
  onClose: () => void;
  existingCertificadoUrl?: string;
  existingAvaliacao?: { notaCurso?: number; notaPlataforma?: number; notaMaterial?: number; comentario?: string };
  onSubmit: (
    file: File | null,
    evalData: { notaCurso?: number; notaPlataforma?: number; notaMaterial?: number; comentario?: string },
    originSession?: AuthSessionSnapshot,
    onFileUploaded?: () => void
  ) => Promise<void>;
}>;

function StarRating({ value, onChange, labelledBy }: { value: number | undefined; onChange: (v: number) => void; labelledBy: string }) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(0);
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-[4px]" role="group" aria-labelledby={labelledBy}>
      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
        <button
          key={star}
          type="button"
          aria-label={`Avaliar com ${star} de 5 estrelas`}
          aria-pressed={value === star}
          onClick={() => onChange(star)}
          onMouseEnter={() => setHover(star)}
          onMouseLeave={() => setHover(0)}
          className="p-1 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-700 transition-transform hover:scale-110"
        >
          <Star
            aria-hidden="true"
            className={`w-[28px] h-[28px] transition-colors ${
              star <= (hover || value || 0)
                ? "fill-yellow-400 text-yellow-400"
                : "text-gray-200"
            }`}
          />
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function UploadCertificadoModal({ onClose, existingCertificadoUrl, existingAvaliacao, onSubmit }: UploadCertificadoModalProps) {
  const [evalLoading, setEvalLoading] = useState(false);
  const [evalData, setEvalData] = useState<{ notaCurso?: number; notaPlataforma?: number; notaMaterial?: number; comentario?: string }>(existingAvaliacao || {});
  const [certificateFile, setCertificateFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [uploadedCertificateUrl, setUploadedCertificateUrl] = useState<string | undefined>(existingCertificadoUrl);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const submitAttemptRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (existingCertificadoUrl) {
      setUploadedCertificateUrl(existingCertificadoUrl);
    }
  }, [existingCertificadoUrl]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    lockScroll();
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      submitAttemptRef.current += 1;
      unlockScroll();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleCloseModal = () => {
    submitAttemptRef.current += 1;
    onClose();
  };

  useModalAccessibility(modalRef, true, handleCloseModal);

  const effectiveExistingUrl = uploadedCertificateUrl || existingCertificadoUrl;
  const hasValidCertificate = Boolean(certificateFile || effectiveExistingUrl);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (evalLoading) return;
    setErrorMsg(null);
    if (!hasValidCertificate) {
      setErrorMsg("Selecione o arquivo do certificado para enviar.");
      return;
    }
    if (certificateFile && certificateFile.size > 1 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setErrorMsg("O arquivo excede o limite máximo de 1MB.");
      return;
    }
    
    if (!evalData.notaCurso || !evalData.notaPlataforma || !evalData.notaMaterial) {
      setErrorMsg("Por favor, avalie todas as opções usando as estrelas.");
      return;
    }

    const originSession = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(originSession)) {
      setErrorMsg("Sua sessão não está mais ativa. Faça login novamente.");
      return;
    }

    const attemptId = ++submitAttemptRef.current;
    const rawFileAtStart = certificateFile;
    setEvalLoading(true);
    try {
      const optimizedFile = rawFileAtStart ? await compressImage(rawFileAtStart) : null;
      if (!isMountedRef.current || submitAttemptRef.current !== attemptId || !isAuthSessionCurrent(originSession)) {
        return;
      }
      await onSubmit(optimizedFile, evalData, originSession, () => {
        if (isMountedRef.current && submitAttemptRef.current === attemptId && isAuthSessionCurrent(originSession)) {
          setUploadedCertificateUrl((prev) => prev || 'uploaded');
          setCertificateFile((current) => {
            if (current === rawFileAtStart) {
              if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
              return null;
            }
            return current;
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      if (isMountedRef.current && submitAttemptRef.current === attemptId && isAuthSessionCurrent(originSession)) {
        setErrorMsg(err?.message || "Ocorreu um erro ao enviar o certificado.");
      }
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current && submitAttemptRef.current === attemptId) {
        setEvalLoading(false);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div
        ref={modalRef} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="upload-cert-modal-title" tabIndex={-1}
        className="bg-white rounded-3xl w-full max-w-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-200"
      >
        <div className="px-8 py-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between shrink-0 bg-gray-50/50">
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h2 id="upload-cert-modal-title" className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900">Enviar Certificado</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Avalie sua experiência e anexe seu documento</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button" onClick={handleCloseModal} aria-label="Fechar janela de envio de certificado"
            className="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 hover:bg-white rounded-full transition-colors shadow-sm border border-transparent hover:border-gray-200 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-700 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-8 overflow-y-auto">
          <form id="upload-cert-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-[32px]">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-[20px]">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <span id="label-nota-curso" className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
                  Como você avalia a qualidade e relevância do conteúdo do curso?
                </span>
                <StarRating labelledBy="label-nota-curso" value={evalData.notaCurso} onChange={(v) => setEvalData({ ...evalData, notaCurso: v })} />
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-px bg-gray-100" />
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <span id="label-nota-plataforma" className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
                  Como foi sua experiência com a plataforma de estudos (AVA) da instituição participante?
                </span>
                <StarRating labelledBy="label-nota-plataforma" value={evalData.notaPlataforma} onChange={(v) => setEvalData({ ...evalData, notaPlataforma: v })} />
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-px bg-gray-100" />
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <span id="label-nota-material" className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
                  Como você avalia os materiais de apoio e recursos didáticos?
                </span>
                <StarRating labelledBy="label-nota-material" value={evalData.notaMaterial} onChange={(v) => setEvalData({ ...evalData, notaMaterial: v })} />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-[8px]">
              <label htmlFor="cert-comentario" className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
                Gostaria de deixar algum comentário, elogio ou sugestão?
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="cert-comentario" rows={3} maxLength={500}
                className="w-full px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 focus:bg-white transition-all text-gray-800 resize-none"
                placeholder="Opcional. Nos conte mais sobre sua experiência..."
                value={evalData.comentario || ''}
                onChange={e => setEvalData({...evalData, comentario: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-[8px]">
              <label htmlFor="certificado-file" className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
                Anexe seu certificado oficial
              </label>
              <div className="relative group mt-1">
                <input
                  ref={fileInputRef} id="certificado-file" name="certificado-file" type="file"
                  disabled={evalLoading} required={!effectiveExistingUrl} accept="application/pdf,image/png,image/jpeg"
                  onChange={e => {
                    if (evalLoading) return;
                    setErrorMsg(null);
                    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                    if (file) {
                      const allowedTypes = ['application/pdf', 'image/png', 'image/jpeg'];
                      if (!allowedTypes.includes(file.type)) {
                        setErrorMsg("Formato inválido. Selecione um arquivo PDF, PNG ou JPEG.");
                        e.target.value = '';
                        setCertificateFile(null);
                        return;
                      }
                      if (file.size > 1 * 1024 * 1024) {
                        setErrorMsg("O arquivo excede o limite máximo de 1MB.");
                        e.target.value = '';
                        setCertificateFile(null);
                        return;
                      }
                      setCertificateFile(file);
                    } else {
                      setCertificateFile(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full opacity-0 cursor-pointer z-10 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                />
                <div className={`w-full p-8 border-2 border-dashed rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-[12px] transition-colors ${hasValidCertificate ? 'border-blue-300 bg-blue-50' : 'border-gray-300 bg-gray-50 group-hover:bg-gray-100 group-hover:border-gray-400'}`}>
                  <div className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${hasValidCertificate ? 'bg-blue-100' : 'bg-gray-200 group-hover:bg-gray-300'} transition-colors`}>
                    <UploadCloud className={`w-7 h-7 ${hasValidCertificate ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-gray-500'}`} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center">
                    {certificateFile ? (
                      <>
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-900">{certificateFile.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">{(certificateFile.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB</p>
                      </>
                    ) : effectiveExistingUrl ? (
                      <>
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-900">Certificado já carregado no sistema</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">Clique aqui apenas se desejar substituir o arquivo.</p>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Clique para selecionar ou arraste o arquivo</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Tamanho máximo de 1MB. Formatos: PDF, PNG, JPG.</p>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {errorMsg && (
              <div role="alert" className="p-4 bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm rounded-xl border border-red-100 text-center font-bold shadow-sm">
                {errorMsg}
              </div>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>

        <div className="px-8 py-5 border-t border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-[12px] shrink-0 justify-end">
          <button type="button" onClick={handleCloseModal} className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full text-gray-600 font-semibold text-sm hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors">
            Cancelar
          </button>
          <button
            type="submit" form="upload-cert-form" disabled={evalLoading || !hasValidCertificate}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto px-8 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white font-bold text-sm hover:bg-[#1e40af] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-blue-900/20"
          >
            {evalLoading ? 'Enviando...' : 'Enviar para Análise'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/UploadCertificadoModal.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/VagaDetalheModal.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { X, MapPin, Building2, Clock, DollarSign, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import { Vaga } from '../../store/useVagasStore';
import { useImagensStore } from '../../store/useImagensStore';
import { getVagaImageFallback, getVagaColorFallback } from '../../lib/vagasImages';
import { formatTime, formatCurrency, parseMonetaryValue } from '../../lib/formatters';
import { lockScroll, unlockScroll, useModalAccessibility } from '../../lib/modalScroll';

type VagaDetalheModalProps = Readonly<{
  vagaDetalhe: Vaga;
  isCandidatado: boolean;
  loadingJornadaVaga: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onQueroConcorrer: (vaga: Vaga) => void;
}>;

export function VagaDetalheModal({ vagaDetalhe, isCandidatado, loadingJornadaVaga, onClose, onQueroConcorrer }: VagaDetalheModalProps) {
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();
  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    lockScroll();
    return () => unlockScroll();
  }, []);
  useModalAccessibility(modalRef, true, onClose);

  const parseBolsa = (valor: any) => parseMonetaryValue(valor);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6">

      <div 
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      <div
        ref={modalRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="vaga-detalhe-modal-title"
        tabIndex={-1}
        className="relative w-full max-w-4xl bg-white rounded-[16px] shadow-2xl hc-border animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden"
      >

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-6 border-b border-gray-100 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-10">
          <h2 id="vaga-detalhe-modal-title" className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 tracking-tight">Detalhes da Oportunidade</h2>
          <button 
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose} 
            className="p-2 bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200 hover:text-gray-700 rounded-full transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-700 focus:outline-none"
            aria-label="Fechar"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-y-auto p-6 flex flex-col gap-8">

          <div 
            className="relative w-full aspect-video md:aspect-[21/9] rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm flex shrink-0"
            style={{ backgroundColor: getVagaColorFallback(vagaDetalhe.codigoDaVaga || vagaDetalhe.id) }}
          >

            <div className="absolute -top-16 -left-16 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
            <div className="absolute bottom-8 left-[35%] w-24 h-24 rounded-full border-4 border-white/10" />

            <div className="relative z-10 w-[55%] p-8 flex flex-col justify-center gap-[12px]">
              <span className="text-white font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl leading-snug drop-shadow-md capitalize mb-[12px]">
                {vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga ? vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga.toLowerCase() : 'Oportunidade'}
              </span>

              {vagaDetalhe.cidade && (
                <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-sm md:text-base font-medium gap-[8px]">
                  <MapPin className="w-5 h-[20px] shrink-0" />
                  <span className="truncate">{vagaDetalhe.cidade}</span>
                </div>
              )}

                  {vagaDetalhe.horarioEntrada && vagaDetalhe.horarioSaida && (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-sm md:text-base font-medium gap-[8px]">
                      <Clock className="w-5 h-[20px] shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        {formatTime(vagaDetalhe.horarioEntrada)} às {formatTime(vagaDetalhe.horarioSaida)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTAGIO') || vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTÁGIO')) && parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0) ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-sm md:text-base font-medium gap-[8px]">
                      <DollarSign className="w-5 h-[20px] shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ) : ((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('APRENDIZ')) && parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato) > 0) ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-sm md:text-base font-medium gap-[8px]">
                      <DollarSign className="w-5 h-[20px] shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ) : ((parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 || parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato) > 0)) && (
                    <div className="flex items-center text-white/90 text-sm md:text-base font-medium gap-[8px]">
                      <DollarSign className="w-5 h-[20px] shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        R$ {parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 ? formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata)) : formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
            </div>

            <div className="relative z-10 w-[45%] h-full shrink-0">
              <img 
                src={vagaDetalhe.image || getVagaImageFallback(vagaDetalhe.codigoDaVaga || vagaDetalhe.id, imagensPublicas)} 
                alt={vagaDetalhe.areaProfissional} 
                className="w-full h-full object-cover border-l-[3px] border-white/10"
                referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-6 leading-tight">
              {vagaDetalhe.areaProfissional || vagaDetalhe.areaDeAtuacao || 'Vaga Confidencial'}
            </h3>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-[12px]">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 rounded-full text-sm font-medium">
                <Building2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                {vagaDetalhe.nomeDaEmpresa || 'Confidencial'}
              </div>
              {vagaDetalhe.instituicaoCooperada && (
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 rounded-full text-sm font-medium">
                  <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                  {vagaDetalhe.instituicaoCooperada}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-[24px]">
            {vagaDetalhe.descricao && (
              <section>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-[12px]">Descrição</h4>
                <p className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed text-base">{vagaDetalhe.descricao}</p>
              </section>
            )}

            {vagaDetalhe.atividades && (
              <section>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-[12px]">Atividades</h4>
                <p className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed text-base">{vagaDetalhe.atividades}</p>
              </section>
            )}

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-[24px]">
              <section>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-[12px]">Requisitos</h4>
                <p className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed text-base">
                  {(() => {
                    const i = vagaDetalhe.semestreInicio;
                    const f = vagaDetalhe.semestreFinal;
                    if (i && f && i === f) return `Cursando o ${i}º semestre.`;
                    if (i && f) return `Cursando do ${i}º ao ${f}º semestre.`;
                    if (i) return `A partir do ${i}º semestre.`;
                    if (f) return `Até o ${f}º semestre.`;
                    return 'Não informado';
                  })()}
                </p>
              </section>
              {(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 || parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato) > 0 || vagaDetalhe.beneficios) && (
                <section>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-[12px]">
                    {vagaDetalhe.beneficios 
                      ? ((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('APRENDIZ')) ? 'Salário / Benefícios' : 'Bolsa / Benefícios')
                      : ((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('APRENDIZ')) ? 'Salário' : (vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTAGIO') || vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTÁGIO')) ? 'Bolsa' : 'Remuneração')}
                  </h4>
                  <div className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed text-base flex flex-col gap-1">
                    {(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 || parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato) > 0) && (
                      <p>
                        {((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTAGIO') || vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('ESTÁGIO')) && parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0) ? (
                          <>R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : ''}</>
                        ) : ((vagaDetalhe.tipoDeVaga?.toUpperCase().includes('APRENDIZ')) && parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato) > 0) ? (
                          <>R$ {formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}</>
                        ) : (
                          <>R$ {parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata) > 0 ? formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.bolsaAuxilioExata)) : formatCurrency(parseBolsa(vagaDetalhe.salarioExato))} {vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoAuxilioBolsa}` : vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario ? `/ ${vagaDetalhe.tipoDeSalario}` : ''}</>
                        )}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {vagaDetalhe.beneficios && (
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-1">{vagaDetalhe.beneficios}</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 p-6 flex justify-end shrink-0">
          {isCandidatado ? (
             <button
                disabled
               className="bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 px-8 py-[12px] rounded-full font-medium cursor-not-allowed w-full sm:w-auto"
             >
               Você já está participando desta vaga
             </button>
          ) : (
            <button 
              onClick={() => onQueroConcorrer(vagaDetalhe)}
              disabled={loadingJornadaVaga}
              className="bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white px-8 py-[12px] rounded-full font-semibold hover:bg-[#1e40af] transition-all duration-300 disabled:opacity-50 shadow-sm w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              {loadingJornadaVaga ? 'Processando...' : 'Quero Participar'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/VagaDetalheModal.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/VLibrasWidget.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

declare global {
  interface Window {
    VLibras?: {
      Widget: new (url: string) => unknown;
    };
  }
}

const VLIBRAS_SCRIPT_SRC = 'https://vlibras.gov.br/app/vlibras-plugin.js';
const VLIBRAS_SRI_HASH = 'sha384-SCil2fuOCOLYO4bxpOW27F/feaFLCHDhc5HDDad0feHUHd0Q6+Wye9e/8Lq09Vqj';

export function VLibrasWidget() {
  const initializedRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initializedRef.current) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    let idleCallbackId: number | null = null;
    let initTimeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    let retryTimeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    const instantiateWidget = () => {
      if (!isMounted || initializedRef.current) return;
      if (window.VLibras) {
        try {
          new window.VLibras.Widget('https://vlibras.gov.br/app');
          initializedRef.current = true;
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[VLibras] Erro ao instanciar o widget:', err);
        }
      }
    };

    const createVerifiedScript = (id: string, allowRetry: boolean): HTMLScriptElement => {
      const el = document.createElement('script');
      el.id = id;
      el.src = VLIBRAS_SCRIPT_SRC;
      el.async = true;
      el.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      el.integrity = VLIBRAS_SRI_HASH;
      el.onload = instantiateWidget;
      el.onerror = () => {
        el.remove();
        if (!isMounted) return;
        if (allowRetry) {
          retryTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!isMounted || initializedRef.current) return;
            if (!document.getElementById(id)) {
              document.body.appendChild(createVerifiedScript(id, false));
            }
          }, 1500);
        } else {
          console.warn('[AppSec] Falha ao validar integridade SRI ou rede do script VLibras.');
        }
      };
      return el;
    };

    const initVLibras = () => {
      if (!isMounted || initializedRef.current) return;
      const scriptId = 'vlibras-plugin-script';
      const existingScript = document.getElementById(scriptId) as HTMLScriptElement | null;

      if (!existingScript) {
        document.body.appendChild(createVerifiedScript(scriptId, true));
      } else if (window.VLibras) {
        instantiateWidget();
      } else {
        existingScript.addEventListener('load', instantiateWidget, { once: true });
      }
    };

    const onReady = () => {
      if (!isMounted) return;
      if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
        idleCallbackId = window.requestIdleCallback(() => initVLibras(), { timeout: 4000 });
      } else {
        initTimeoutId = setTimeout(initVLibras, 2500);
      }
    };

    const events = ['mousedown', 'keydown', 'touchstart', 'scroll'];
    const onUserInteraction = () => {
      initVLibras();
      events.forEach((e) => window.removeEventListener(e, onUserInteraction));
    };
    events.forEach((e) => window.addEventListener(e, onUserInteraction, { once: true, passive: true }));

    if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
      onReady();
    } else {
      window.addEventListener('load', onReady, { once: true });
    }

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      window.removeEventListener('load', onReady);
      events.forEach((e) => window.removeEventListener(e, onUserInteraction));
      if (idleCallbackId !== null && 'cancelIdleCallback' in window) {
        window.cancelIdleCallback(idleCallbackId);
      }
      if (initTimeoutId !== null) {
        clearTimeout(initTimeoutId);
      }
      if (retryTimeoutId !== null) {
        clearTimeout(retryTimeoutId);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div {...({ vw: 'true' } as React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>)} className="enabled">
      <div {...({ 'vw-access-button': 'true' } as React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>)} className="active" />
      <div {...({ 'vw-plugin-wrapper': 'true' } as React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>)}>
        <div className="vw-plugin-top-wrapper" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/components/ui/VLibrasWidget.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useCursosFilters.ts ---
import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useCursosStore, Curso } from '../store/useCursosStore';

export function useCursosFilters() {
  const cursos = useCursosStore((s) => s.cursos);

  const [filtroInstituicao, setFiltroInstituicao] = useState("");
  const [filtroArea, setFiltroArea] = useState("");
  const [filtroCarga, setFiltroCarga] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setFiltroArea("");
    setFiltroCarga("");
  }, [filtroInstituicao]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFiltroCarga("");
  }, [filtroArea]);

  const parseHoras = (val: string | number | undefined | null) => {
    if (val === undefined || val === null || val === '') return 0;
    if (typeof val === 'number') return Math.floor(val);
    const match = String(val).match(/\d+/);
    return match ? parseInt(match[0], 10) : 0;
  };

  const getRange = (horas: number) => {
    if (horas <= 4) return "ate_4";
    if (horas <= 12) return "ate_12";
    if (horas <= 20) return "ate_20";
    if (horas <= 40) return "ate_40";
    if (horas <= 80) return "ate_80";
    return "acima_80";
  };

  const instituicoes = useMemo<readonly string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    cursos.forEach((c) => {
      if (c.instituicaoCooperada) set.add(String(c.instituicaoCooperada).trim().toUpperCase());
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [cursos]);

  const areas = useMemo<readonly string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    cursos.forEach((c) => {
      if (filtroInstituicao && String(c.instituicaoCooperada || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== filtroInstituicao) return;
      const areaVal = c.nomeCineRotulo || c.area;
      if (areaVal) set.add(String(areaVal).trim().toUpperCase());
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [cursos, filtroInstituicao]);

  const cursosFiltradosInstituicao = useMemo(
    () =>
      cursos.filter(
        (c) =>
          !filtroInstituicao || String(c.instituicaoCooperada || '').trim().toUpperCase() === filtroInstituicao,
      ),
    [cursos, filtroInstituicao],
  );

  const cursosFiltradosArea = useMemo(
    () =>
      cursosFiltradosInstituicao.filter(
        (c) => !filtroArea || String(c.nomeCineRotulo || c.area || '').trim().toUpperCase() === filtroArea,
      ),
    [cursosFiltradosInstituicao, filtroArea],
  );

  const cargasDisponiveis = useMemo<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => {
    const ranges = new Set<string>();
    cursosFiltradosArea.forEach((c) => {
      const h = parseHoras(c.cargaHoraria);
      ranges.add(getRange(h));
    });
    return ranges;
  }, [cursosFiltradosArea]);

  const cursosFiltrados = useMemo<readonly Curso[]>(() => {
    return cursosFiltradosArea.filter((curso) => {
      if (!filtroCarga) return true;
      const h = parseHoras(curso.cargaHoraria);
      return getRange(h) === filtroCarga;
    });
  }, [cursosFiltradosArea, filtroCarga]);

  return {
    filtroInstituicao, setFiltroInstituicao,
    filtroArea, setFiltroArea,
    filtroCarga, setFiltroCarga,
    instituicoes, areas, cargasDisponiveis,
    cursosFiltrados
  } as const;
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useCursosFilters.ts ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useCursosPreMatricula.ts ---
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useCursosStore, Curso } from "../store/useCursosStore";
import { useJornadaCursosStore } from "../store/useJornadaCursosStore";
import { getAuthSessionSnapshot, isAuthSessionCurrent } from "../store/useAuthStore";
import { api } from "../api";

export function useCursosPreMatricula() {
  const cursos = useCursosStore((s) => s.cursos);
  const loading = useCursosStore((s) => s.loading);
  const fetchCursos = useCursosStore((s) => s.fetchCursos);

  const jornadaCursos = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.jornadaCursos);

  const addJornadaCursoLocal = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.addJornadaCursoLocal);
  const updateJornadaCursoLocal = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.updateJornadaCursoLocal);

  const [cursoDetalhe, setCursoDetalhe] = useState<Curso | null>(null);
  const [cursoMatricula, setCursoMatricula] = useState<Curso | null>(null);
  const [loadingCursoId, setLoadingCursoId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [waitingReturnForCursoId, setWaitingReturnForCursoId] = useState<string | null>(() => sessionStorage.getItem('crasp_waitingReturnForCursoId'));
  const [activeJornadaId, setActiveJornadaId] = useState<string | null>(() => sessionStorage.getItem('crasp_activeJornadaId'));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (waitingReturnForCursoId) sessionStorage.setItem('crasp_waitingReturnForCursoId', waitingReturnForCursoId);
    else sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_waitingReturnForCursoId');
  }, [waitingReturnForCursoId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeJornadaId) sessionStorage.setItem('crasp_activeJornadaId', activeJornadaId);
    else sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_activeJornadaId');
  }, [activeJornadaId]);

  const [returnModalConfig, setReturnModalConfig] = useState<{
    isOpen: boolean;
    jornadaId: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handlePreMatricula = async (curso: Curso) => {
    if (!curso.id) {
      setErrorMessage("Este curso não possui um identificador válido (ID) no catálogo.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
      return;
    }
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    setLoadingCursoId(curso.id);
    try {
      const tituloResolvido = curso.titulo || curso.tituloEditado || "";
      const cursoSnapshot = {
        titulo: tituloResolvido,
        tituloEditado: curso.tituloEditado || tituloResolvido,
        instituicaoCooperada: curso.instituicaoCooperada || "",
        area: curso.nomeCineRotulo || curso.area || "",
        cargaHoraria: curso.cargaHoraria?.toString(),
        image: curso.image || undefined,
        linkDoCurso: curso.linkDoCurso || curso.link || curso.url || curso.site || curso.linkDeInscricao || undefined
      };

      const resp = await api.jornadaCursos.criar(curso.id, cursoSnapshot);
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;

      addJornadaCursoLocal({
        id: curso.id,
        cursoId: curso.id,
        cursoSnapshot,
        status: resp.status,
      });

      setActiveJornadaId(curso.id);
      setCursoMatricula(curso);
      if (cursoDetalhe) {
        setCursoDetalhe(null);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      console.error(error);
      setErrorMessage("Não foi possível realizar a pré-matrícula neste momento. Tente novamente mais tarde.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
    } finally {
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        setLoadingCursoId(null);
      }
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCursos();
  }, [fetchCursos]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleVisibilityChange = async () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && waitingReturnForCursoId && activeJornadaId) {

        setReturnModalConfig({ isOpen: true, jornadaId: activeJornadaId });

        setWaitingReturnForCursoId(null);
        setActiveJornadaId(null);
        setCursoMatricula(null);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);
  }, [waitingReturnForCursoId, activeJornadaId]);

  const handleConfirmReturn = async (jornadaId: string) => {
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    setReturnModalConfig(null);
    updateJornadaCursoLocal(jornadaId, "MATRICULADO");

    try {
      await api.jornadaCursos.atualizarStatus(jornadaId, "MATRICULADO");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        updateJornadaCursoLocal(jornadaId, "PRE_MATRICULADO");
      }
    }
  };

  const handleCancelReturn = () => {
    setReturnModalConfig(null);
  };

  return {
    cursos,
    loading,
    jornadaCursos,
    cursoDetalhe,
    setCursoDetalhe,
    cursoMatricula,
    setCursoMatricula,
    loadingCursoId,
    setWaitingReturnForCursoId,
    handlePreMatricula,
    returnModalConfig,
    handleConfirmReturn,
    handleCancelReturn,
    errorMessage,
    setErrorMessage
  };
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useCursosPreMatricula.ts ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useDocumentModal.ts ---
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export type DocumentType = 'privacidade' | 'seguranca' | 'termos' | 'cookies';

export const useDocumentModal = (isOpen: boolean, initialDocument: DocumentType = 'privacidade') => {
  const [currentDoc, setCurrentDoc] = useState<DocumentType>(initialDocument);
  const [zoomLevel, setZoomLevel] = useState<number>(1);
  const [isDarkMode, setIsDarkMode] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isHighContrast, setIsHighContrast] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) setCurrentDoc(initialDocument);
  }, [isOpen, initialDocument]);

  const handleZoomIn = () => setZoomLevel(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, 2));
  const handleZoomOut = () => setZoomLevel(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 0));
  const toggleDarkMode = () => setIsDarkMode(prev => !prev);
  const toggleHighContrast = () => setIsHighContrast(prev => !prev);
  
  const handleReset = () => {
    setZoomLevel(1);
    setIsDarkMode(false);
    setIsHighContrast(false);
  };

  const nextDocMap: Record<DocumentType, { type: DocumentType; label: string }> = {
    'privacidade': { type: 'seguranca', label: 'SEGURANÇA' },
    'seguranca': { type: 'termos', label: 'TERMOS DE USO' },
    'termos': { type: 'cookies', label: 'PREFERÊNCIAS' },
    'cookies': { type: 'privacidade', label: 'PRIVACIDADE' },
  };

  const handleNextDoc = () => {
    if (currentDoc === 'privacidade') setCurrentDoc('seguranca');
    else if (currentDoc === 'seguranca') setCurrentDoc('termos');
    else if (currentDoc === 'termos') setCurrentDoc('cookies');
    else setCurrentDoc('privacidade');
  };

  const getDocTitle = () => {
    if (currentDoc === 'privacidade') return 'PRIVACIDADE';
    if (currentDoc === 'seguranca') return 'SEGURANÇA';
    if (currentDoc === 'cookies') return 'PREFERÊNCIAS E SESSÃO';
    return 'TERMOS DE USO';
  };

  const getDocName = () => {
    if (currentDoc === 'privacidade') return 'Aviso de Privacidade do Site do Conselho Regional de Administração de São Paulo (CRA-SP)';
    if (currentDoc === 'seguranca') return 'Política de Segurança';
    if (currentDoc === 'cookies') return 'Preferências de Armazenamento Local';
    return 'Termo de Uso do Site do Conselho Regional de Administração de São Paulo (CRA-SP)';
  };

  return {
    currentDoc, zoomLevel, isDarkMode, isHighContrast,
    handleZoomIn, handleZoomOut, toggleDarkMode, toggleHighContrast,
    handleReset, handleNextDoc, getDocTitle, getDocName, nextDocMap,
  };
};

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useInView.ts ---
import { useState, useEffect, RefObject } from 'react';

export function useInView(ref: RefObject<Element>, options: { once?: boolean; margin?: string } = {}) {
  const [isInView, setIsInView] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          setIsInView(true);
          if (options.once && ref.current) {
            observer.unobserve(ref.current);
          }
        } else if (!options.once) {
          setIsInView(false);
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: options.margin || '0px' }
    );

    observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [ref, options.margin, options.once]);

  return isInView;
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useVagasFilters.ts ---
import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useVagasStore, Vaga } from '../store/useVagasStore';
import { useSearchStore } from '../store/useSearchStore';
import { parseMonetaryValue } from '../lib/formatters';

export function useVagasFilters() {
  const vagas = useVagasStore((s) => s.vagas);
  const { globalSearchTerm: searchTerm } = useSearchStore();
  
  const [filtroEmpresa, setFiltroEmpresa] = useState("");
  const [filtroTipoVaga, setFiltroTipoVaga] = useState("");
  const [filtroCidade, setFiltroCidade] = useState("");
  const [filtroBolsa, setFiltroBolsa] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setFiltroTipoVaga("");
    setFiltroCidade("");
    setFiltroBolsa("");
  }, [filtroEmpresa]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFiltroCidade("");
    setFiltroBolsa("");
  }, [filtroTipoVaga]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFiltroBolsa("");
  }, [filtroCidade]);

  const parseBolsa = (str: string | number | undefined) => parseMonetaryValue(str);

  const getBolsaRange = (valor: number) => {
    if (valor <= 500) return "ate_500";
    if (valor <= 1000) return "ate_1000";
    if (valor <= 1500) return "ate_1500";
    if (valor <= 2000) return "ate_2000";
    return "acima_2000";
  };

  const vagasFiltradasText = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchTerm) return vagas;
    const lowerSearch = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
    return vagas.filter((v) => {
      const tipo = (v.tipoDeVaga || '').toLowerCase();
      const empresa = (v.nomeDaEmpresa || '').toLowerCase();
      const nivel = (v.nivelEscolar || '').toLowerCase();
      const desc = (v.descricao || '').toLowerCase();
      const ativ = (v.atividades || '').toLowerCase();
      
      return tipo.includes(lowerSearch) || 
             empresa.includes(lowerSearch) || 
             nivel.includes(lowerSearch) || 
             desc.includes(lowerSearch) || 
             ativ.includes(lowerSearch);
    });
  }, [vagas, searchTerm]);

  const empresas = useMemo<readonly string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    vagasFiltradasText.forEach((v) => {
      if (v.instituicaoCooperada) set.add(v.instituicaoCooperada.trim().toUpperCase());
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [vagasFiltradasText]);

  const vagasFiltradasEmpresa = useMemo(
    () =>
      vagasFiltradasText.filter((v) => !filtroEmpresa || v.instituicaoCooperada?.trim().toUpperCase() === filtroEmpresa),
    [vagasFiltradasText, filtroEmpresa],
  );

  const tiposDeVaga = useMemo<readonly string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    vagasFiltradasEmpresa.forEach((v) => {
      if (v.tipoDeVaga) set.add(v.tipoDeVaga.trim().toUpperCase());
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [vagasFiltradasEmpresa]);

  const vagasFiltradasTipo = useMemo(
    () =>
      vagasFiltradasEmpresa.filter((v) => !filtroTipoVaga || v.tipoDeVaga?.trim().toUpperCase() === filtroTipoVaga),
    [vagasFiltradasEmpresa, filtroTipoVaga],
  );

  const cidades = useMemo<readonly string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    vagasFiltradasTipo.forEach((v) => {
      if (v.cidade) set.add(v.cidade.trim().toUpperCase());
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [vagasFiltradasTipo]);

  const vagasFiltradasCidade = useMemo(
    () =>
      vagasFiltradasTipo.filter(
        (v) => !filtroCidade || v.cidade?.trim().toUpperCase() === filtroCidade,
      ),
    [vagasFiltradasTipo, filtroCidade],
  );

  const bolsasDisponiveis = useMemo<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => {
    const ranges = new Set<string>();
    vagasFiltradasCidade.forEach((v) => {
      const valBolsa = parseBolsa(v.bolsaAuxilioExata);
      const valSalario = parseBolsa(v.salarioExato);
      const val = valBolsa > 0 ? valBolsa : valSalario;
      if (val > 0) {
        ranges.add(getBolsaRange(val));
      }
    });
    return ranges;
  }, [vagasFiltradasCidade]);

  const vagasFiltradas = useMemo<readonly Vaga[]>(() => {
    return vagasFiltradasCidade.filter((vaga) => {
      if (!filtroBolsa) return true;
      const valBolsa = parseBolsa(vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata);
      const valSalario = parseBolsa(vaga.salarioExato);
      const val = valBolsa > 0 ? valBolsa : valSalario;
      return val > 0 && getBolsaRange(val) === filtroBolsa;
    });
  }, [vagasFiltradasCidade, filtroBolsa]);

  return {
    filtroEmpresa, setFiltroEmpresa,
    filtroTipoVaga, setFiltroTipoVaga,
    filtroCidade, setFiltroCidade,
    filtroBolsa, setFiltroBolsa,
    empresas, tiposDeVaga, cidades, bolsasDisponiveis,
    vagasFiltradas, parseBolsa
  } as const;
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useVagasFilters.ts ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/hooks/useVagasInteresse.ts ---
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useVagasStore, Vaga } from "../store/useVagasStore";
import { useJornadaVagasStore } from "../store/useJornadaVagasStore";
import { getAuthSessionSnapshot, isAuthSessionCurrent } from "../store/useAuthStore";
import { api } from "../api";

export function useVagasInteresse() {
  const vagas = useVagasStore((s) => s.vagas);
  const loading = useVagasStore((s) => s.loading);
  const fetchVagas = useVagasStore((s) => s.fetchVagas);

  const jornadaVagas = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.jornadaVagas);

  const addJornadaVagaLocal = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.addJornadaVagaLocal);
  const updateJornadaVagaLocal = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.updateJornadaVagaLocal);

  const [vagaDetalhe, setVagaDetalhe] = useState<Vaga | null>(null);
  const [vagaInteresse, setVagaInteresse] = useState<Vaga | null>(null);
  const [loadingVagaId, setLoadingVagaId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [waitingReturnForVagaId, setWaitingReturnForVagaId] = useState<string | null>(() => sessionStorage.getItem('crasp_waitingReturnForVagaId'));
  const [activeJornadaVagaId, setActiveJornadaVagaId] = useState<string | null>(() => sessionStorage.getItem('crasp_activeJornadaVagaId'));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (waitingReturnForVagaId) sessionStorage.setItem('crasp_waitingReturnForVagaId', waitingReturnForVagaId);
    else sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_waitingReturnForVagaId');
  }, [waitingReturnForVagaId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeJornadaVagaId) sessionStorage.setItem('crasp_activeJornadaVagaId', activeJornadaVagaId);
    else sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_activeJornadaVagaId');
  }, [activeJornadaVagaId]);

  const [returnModalConfig, setReturnModalConfig] = useState<{
    isOpen: boolean;
    jornadaVagaId: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleQueroConcorrer = async (vaga: Vaga) => {
    if (!vaga.id) return;
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    setLoadingVagaId(vaga.id);
    try {
      const vagaSnapshot = {
        tituloEditado: vaga.areaProfissional || "",
        nomeDaEmpresa: vaga.nomeDaEmpresa || "Confidencial",
        instituicaoCooperada: vaga.instituicaoCooperada || "",
        bolsaAuxilioExata: vaga.bolsaAuxilioExata,
        image: vaga.image || undefined,
        linkDeInscricao: vaga.linkDeInscricao || undefined,
        localidade: vaga.localidade || undefined,
        tipoDeVaga: vaga.tipoDeVaga || undefined
      };

      const resp = await api.jornadaVagas.criar(vaga.id, vagaSnapshot);
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;

      addJornadaVagaLocal({
        id: vaga.id,
        vagaId: vaga.id,
        vagaSnapshot,
        status: resp.status,
      });

      setActiveJornadaVagaId(vaga.id);
      setVagaInteresse(vaga);
      if (vagaDetalhe) {
        setVagaDetalhe(null);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      console.error(error);
      setErrorMessage("Não foi possível processar sua participação neste momento. Tente novamente mais tarde.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
    } finally {
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        setLoadingVagaId(null);
      }
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchVagas();
  }, [fetchVagas]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleVisibilityChange = async () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && waitingReturnForVagaId && activeJornadaVagaId) {
        setReturnModalConfig({ isOpen: true, jornadaVagaId: activeJornadaVagaId });

        setWaitingReturnForVagaId(null);
        setActiveJornadaVagaId(null);
        setVagaInteresse(null);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);
  }, [waitingReturnForVagaId, activeJornadaVagaId]);

  const handleConfirmReturn = async (jornadaVagaId: string) => {
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    setReturnModalConfig(null);
    updateJornadaVagaLocal(jornadaVagaId, "CANDIDATADO");

    try {
      await api.jornadaVagas.atualizarStatus(jornadaVagaId, "CANDIDATADO");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        updateJornadaVagaLocal(jornadaVagaId, "PRE_CANDIDATADO");
      }
    }
  };

  const handleCancelReturn = () => {
    setReturnModalConfig(null);
  };

  return {
    vagas,
    loading,
    jornadaVagas,
    vagaDetalhe,
    setVagaDetalhe,
    vagaInteresse,
    setVagaInteresse,
    loadingVagaId,
    setWaitingReturnForVagaId,
    handleQueroConcorrer,
    returnModalConfig,
    handleConfirmReturn,
    handleCancelReturn,
    errorMessage,
    setErrorMessage
  };
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/lib/compressor.ts ---
export const compressImage = async (file: File): Promise<File> => {

  if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) return file;

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.src = event.target?.result as string;
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        const MAX_WIDTH = 1920;
        const MAX_HEIGHT = 1920;
        let width = img.width;
        let height = img.height;

        if (width > height) {
          if (width > MAX_WIDTH) {
            height *= MAX_WIDTH / width;
            width = MAX_WIDTH;
          }
        } else {
          if (height > MAX_HEIGHT) {
            width *= MAX_HEIGHT / height;
            height = MAX_HEIGHT;
          }
        }

        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) return resolve(file); 

        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

        canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
          if (blob) {
            const compressedFile = new File([blob], file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "") + ".jpg", {
              type: 'image/jpeg',
              lastModified: Date.now(),
            });
            resolve(compressedFile);
          } else {
            resolve(file); 
          }
        }, 'image/jpeg', 0.7);
      };
      img.onerror = () => resolve(file); 
    };
    reader.onerror = () => resolve(file); 
  });
};

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/lib/formatters.ts ---
export const formatTime = (timeStr?: string) => {
  if (!timeStr) return '';
  const match = timeStr.match(/(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d/);
  if (match) {
    return match[0];
  }
  return timeStr.substring(0, 5);
};

export const formatCargaHoraria = (carga?: string | number) => {
  if (carga === undefined || carga === null || carga === '') return '';
  const str = String(carga).trim();
  const match = str.match(/^(\d{1,3}):(\d{2})$/);
  if (match) {
    const hours = parseInt(match[1], 10);
    const minutes = parseInt(match[2], 10);
    
    if (minutes === 0) {
      return `${hours} hora${hours !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    }
    return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;
  }
  if (/^\d+$/.test(str)) {
    return `${str}h`;
  }
  return str;
};

export const parseMonetaryValue = (raw?: string | number | null): number => {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === null || raw === '') return 0;
  if (typeof raw === 'number') {
    return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw > 0 ? raw : 0;
  }
  const match = String(raw).match(/\d[0-9.,]*\d|\d/);
  if (!match) return 0;
  const token = match[0];
  let normalized = token;
  if (token.includes('.') && token.includes(',')) {
    if (token.lastIndexOf(',') > token.lastIndexOf('.')) {
      normalized = token.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.');
    } else {
      normalized = token.replace(/,/g, '');
    }
  } else if (token.includes(',')) {
    normalized = token.replace(',', '.');
  } else if (token.includes('.')) {
    const parts = token.split('.');
    if (parts.length > 2 || parts[1].length === 3) {
      normalized = token.replace(/\./g, '');
    }
  }
  const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : 0;
};

export const formatCurrency = (valor?: number | string | null) => {
  const valorNum = parseMonetaryValue(valor);
  if (!valorNum || valorNum <= 0) return '';
  return valorNum.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
};

export const getInstitutionLogo = (instituicoes: any[], targetName?: string) => {
  if (!targetName || !instituicoes || !instituicoes.length) return null;
  
  const normalize = (str: string) => str.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  const targetNorm = normalize(targetName);
  const getInstName = (inst: any) => inst.instituicao || inst.nomeDaInstituicao || inst.sigla || inst.instituicaoCooperada || '';

  let match = instituicoes.find(inst => getInstName(inst) === targetName);

  if (!match) {
    match = instituicoes.find(inst => normalize(getInstName(inst)) === targetNorm);
  }

  if (!match) {
    match = instituicoes.find(inst => {
      const instNorm = normalize(getInstName(inst));
      return instNorm.length > 2 && (targetNorm.includes(instNorm) || instNorm.includes(targetNorm));
    });
  }

  return match?.logoUrl || match?.image || null;
};

export const optimizeImageUrl = (url?: string, _width = 800, _height?: number) => {
  const fallbackSvg = 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciIHdpZHRoPSIxMDAlIiBoZWlnaHQ9IjEwMCUiPjxyZWN0IHdpZHRoPSIxMDAlIiBoZWlnaHQ9IjEwMCUiIGZpbGw9IiNmM2Y0ZjYiLz48dGV4dCB4PSI1MCUiIHk9IjUwJSIgZm9udC1mYW1pbHk9InNhbnMtc2VyaWYiIGZvbnQtc2l6ZT0iMTQiIGZpbGw9IiM5Y2EzYWYiIGRvbWluYW50LWJhc2VsaW5lPSJtaWRkbGUiIHRleHQtYW5jaG9yPSJtaWRkbGUiPkltYWdlbSBuw6NvIGRpc3BvbsOtdmVsPC90ZXh0Pjwvc3ZnPg==';

  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string' || url.trim() === '' || url === 'undefined' || url === 'null') {
    return fallbackSvg;
  }

  if (url.startsWith('data:') || url.endsWith('.svg') || url.startsWith('/')) {
    return url;
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(url.trim());
    if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:') return fallbackSvg;
    return parsed.href;
  } catch {
    return fallbackSvg;
  }
};

export const toTitleCase = (str: string) => {
  if (!str) return '';
  const lower = str.toLowerCase();
  const prep = ['de', 'da', 'do', 'das', 'dos', 'e'];
  return lower.split(' ').map((word, index) => {
    if (word.length === 0) return word;
    if (index !== 0 && prep.includes(word)) {
      return word;
    }
    return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1);
  }).join(' ');
};

export const mapNivelEscolar = (nivel?: string) => {
  if (!nivel) return null;
  const upper = nivel.toUpperCase();
  if (upper === 'SU') return 'Ensino Superior';
  if (upper === 'EM') return 'Ensino Médio';
  if (upper === 'T' || upper === 'TE') return 'Ensino Técnico';
  return nivel;
};

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/lib/modalScroll.ts ---
import { useEffect, useRef, RefObject } from 'react';

let scrollLockCount = 0;
let originalOverflow = '';
let originalPaddingRight = '';
const focusReturnStack: (HTMLElement | null)[] = [];
const activeModalStack: HTMLElement[] = [];
const pendingTriggerByModal = new WeakMap<HTMLElement, HTMLElement | null>();
const lastKnownTriggerByModal = new WeakMap<HTMLElement, HTMLElement | null>();
let pendingFocusReturnTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let pendingFocusReturnEl: HTMLElement | null = null;

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR =
  'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

function isElementConnected(el: HTMLElement | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!el) return false;
  if (typeof el.isConnected === 'boolean') return el.isConnected;
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && typeof document.contains === 'function') {
    return document.contains(el);
  }
  return true;
}

function isElementInside(container: HTMLElement, target: Element | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!target) return false;
  if (container === target) return true;
  if (typeof container.contains === 'function') {
    return container.contains(target);
  }
  let current: Node | null = target;
  while (current) {
    if (current === container) return true;
    current = current.parentNode;
  }
  return false;
}

function isElementVisible(el: HTMLElement, stopAt?: HTMLElement): boolean {
  let current: HTMLElement | null = el;
  while (current) {
    if (current.hidden || current.getAttribute?.('aria-hidden') === 'true') return false;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.getComputedStyle === 'function') {
      const style = window.getComputedStyle(current);
      if (style.display === 'none' || style.visibility === 'hidden') return false;
    }
    if (stopAt && current === stopAt) break;
    current = current.parentElement;
  }
  return true;
}

function isElementFocusable(el: HTMLElement | null | undefined, stopAt?: HTMLElement): boolean {
  if (!el || typeof el.focus !== 'function' || !isElementConnected(el)) return false;
  if ((el as HTMLButtonElement).disabled === true) return false;
  if (typeof el.getAttribute === 'function' && el.getAttribute('disabled') !== null) return false;
  return isElementVisible(el, stopAt);
}

function getVisibleFocusables(modal: HTMLElement): HTMLElement[] {
  if (typeof modal.querySelectorAll !== 'function') return [];
  return Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR)).filter(el =>
    isElementFocusable(el, modal)
  );
}

function focusFirstInModal(modal: HTMLElement) {
  const focusables = getVisibleFocusables(modal);
  if (focusables.length > 0) {
    focusables[0].focus();
    return;
  }
  if (typeof modal.focus === 'function') {
    modal.focus();
  }
}

function restoreModalOrTriggerFocus(previousEl: HTMLElement | null) {
  const topModal = activeModalStack[activeModalStack.length - 1];
  if (topModal) {
    if (isElementFocusable(previousEl) && isElementInside(topModal, previousEl)) {
      previousEl!.focus();
      return;
    }
    const currentActive = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.activeElement : null;
    if (!isElementInside(topModal, currentActive)) {
      focusFirstInModal(topModal);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (isElementFocusable(previousEl)) {
    previousEl!.focus();
  }
}

function consumePendingFocusReturn(): { hadTimer: boolean; element: HTMLElement | null } {
  if (pendingFocusReturnTimer !== null) {
    clearTimeout(pendingFocusReturnTimer);
    pendingFocusReturnTimer = null;
    const el = pendingFocusReturnEl;
    pendingFocusReturnEl = null;
    return { hadTimer: true, element: el };
  }
  return { hadTimer: false, element: null };
}

export function lockScroll() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  const pending = consumePendingFocusReturn();
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    const topModal = activeModalStack[activeModalStack.length - 1];
    if (activeModalStack.length > scrollLockCount && topModal) {
      if (pendingTriggerByModal.has(topModal)) {
        focusReturnStack.push(pendingTriggerByModal.get(topModal) ?? null);
        pendingTriggerByModal.delete(topModal);
      } else {
        focusReturnStack.push(pending.hadTimer ? pending.element : (lastKnownTriggerByModal.get(topModal) ?? null));
      }
    } else if (pending.hadTimer) {
      focusReturnStack.push(pending.element);
    } else {
      focusReturnStack.push(document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null);
    }
  }
  if (scrollLockCount === 0) {
    const scrollBarWidth = window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    originalPaddingRight = document.body.style.paddingRight;

    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    if (scrollBarWidth > 0) {
      document.body.style.paddingRight = `${scrollBarWidth}px`;
    }
  }
  scrollLockCount++;
}

export function unlockScroll() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  scrollLockCount--;
  if (scrollLockCount <= 0) {
    scrollLockCount = 0;
    document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow || '';
    document.body.style.paddingRight = originalPaddingRight || '';
  }
  const previousEl = focusReturnStack.pop() ?? null;
  if (pendingFocusReturnTimer !== null) {
    clearTimeout(pendingFocusReturnTimer);
  }
  pendingFocusReturnEl = previousEl;
  pendingFocusReturnTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    pendingFocusReturnTimer = null;
    pendingFocusReturnEl = null;
    try {
      restoreModalOrTriggerFocus(previousEl);
    } catch {}
  }, 0);
}

function trapTabNavigation(e: KeyboardEvent, container: HTMLElement) {
  const currentFocusables = getVisibleFocusables(container);
  if (currentFocusables.length === 0) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (typeof container.focus === 'function') container.focus();
    return;
  }
  const first = currentFocusables[0];
  const last = currentFocusables[currentFocusables.length - 1];
  const activeEl = document.activeElement;
  if (!isElementInside(container, activeEl)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    (e.shiftKey ? last : first).focus();
    return;
  }
  if (e.shiftKey && activeEl === first) {
    e.preventDefault();
    last.focus();
  } else if (!e.shiftKey && activeEl === last) {
    e.preventDefault();
    first.focus();
  }
}

function recordModalTriggerBeforeFocus(container: HTMLElement) {
  const pending = consumePendingFocusReturn();
  const candidate = pending.hadTimer
    ? pending.element
    : (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null);

  if (candidate && !isElementInside(container, candidate)) {
    lastKnownTriggerByModal.set(container, candidate);
  }
  if (scrollLockCount <= activeModalStack.length) {
    const resolved =
      candidate && !isElementInside(container, candidate)
        ? candidate
        : (lastKnownTriggerByModal.get(container) ?? null);
    pendingTriggerByModal.set(container, resolved);
  }
}

export function useModalAccessibility(
  containerRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>,
  isOpen: boolean,
  onClose: () => void
) {
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);
  onCloseRef.current = onClose;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || typeof document === 'undefined') return;
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    recordModalTriggerBeforeFocus(container);
    activeModalStack.push(container);
    focusFirstInModal(container);

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const topModal = activeModalStack[activeModalStack.length - 1];
      if (topModal && topModal !== container) return;

      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        onCloseRef.current();
      } else if (e.key === 'Tab') {
        trapTabNavigation(e, container);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
      pendingTriggerByModal.delete(container);
      const idx = activeModalStack.lastIndexOf(container);
      if (idx !== -1) activeModalStack.splice(idx, 1);
    };
  }, [isOpen, containerRef]);
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/lib/vagasImages.ts ---
export function getVagaImageFallback(vagaId?: string | number, dynamicList?: string[]): string {
  if (!dynamicList || dynamicList.length === 0) {
    return 'data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7';
  }

  const idStr = String(vagaId || 'default');
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < idStr.length; i++) {
    hash = idStr.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
  }

  hash = Math.abs(hash);
  return dynamicList[hash % dynamicList.length];
}

export const VAGAS_COLORS = [
  '#004e9a',
  '#0369a1',
  '#0f766e',
  '#1d4ed8',
];

export function getVagaColorFallback(vagaId?: string | number): string {
  if (!vagaId) return VAGAS_COLORS[0];

  const idStr = String(vagaId);
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < idStr.length; i++) {
    hash = idStr.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
  }

  hash = Math.abs(hash);
  return VAGAS_COLORS[hash % VAGAS_COLORS.length];
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/lib/validators.ts ---
export const formatCPF = (value: string) => {
  let v = value.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (v.length > 11) v = v.substring(0, 11);
  if (v.length > 9) {
    v = v.replace(/(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{1,2})/, '$1.$2.$3-$4');
  } else if (v.length > 6) {
    v = v.replace(/(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{1,3})/, '$1.$2.$3');
  } else if (v.length > 3) {
    v = v.replace(/(\d{3})(\d{1,3})/, '$1.$2');
  }
  return v;
};

export const formatPhone = (value: string) => {
  let v = value.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (v.length > 11) v = v.substring(0, 11);
  if (v.length > 10) {
    v = v.replace(/^(\d{2})(\d{5})(\d{4})/, '($1) $2-$3');
  } else if (v.length > 6) {
    v = v.replace(/^(\d{2})(\d{4})(\d{0,4})/, '($1) $2-$3');
  } else if (v.length > 2) {
    v = v.replace(/^(\d{2})(\d{0,5})/, '($1) $2');
  }
  return v;
};

export const sanitizeUrlForOpenRedirect = (url: string | undefined | null): string => {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return '#';
  try {
    const trimmed = url.trim();
    if (!trimmed.startsWith('https://')) return '#';
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
    if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:' || !parsed.hostname) return '#';
    if (parsed.href.includes('javascript:') || parsed.href.includes('data:')) return '#';
    return parsed.href;
  } catch {
    return '#';
  }
};

export const validateCPF = (cpf: string) => {

  const strCPF = cpf.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (strCPF.length !== 11) return false;
  if (/^(\d)\1{10}$/.test(strCPF)) return false;

  let soma = 0;
  let resto;

  for (let i = 1; i <= 9; i++) soma += parseInt(strCPF.substring(i - 1, i)) * (11 - i);
  resto = (soma * 10) % 11;
  if (resto === 10 || resto === 11) resto = 0;
  if (resto !== parseInt(strCPF.substring(9, 10))) return false;

  soma = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) soma += parseInt(strCPF.substring(i - 1, i)) * (12 - i);
  resto = (soma * 10) % 11;
  if (resto === 10 || resto === 11) resto = 0;
  if (resto !== parseInt(strCPF.substring(10, 11))) return false;

  return true;
};
