import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuthStore, getAuthSessionSnapshot, isAuthSessionCurrent } from '../store/useAuthStore';
import { useMecStore } from '../store/useMecStore';
import { api } from '../api';
import { validateCPF } from '../lib/validators';
import { ProfileFormFields } from '../components/ui/ProfileFormFields';
import { DocumentType } from '../hooks/useDocumentModal';
import { DocumentModal } from '../components/ui/DocumentModal';
import { CompleteProfileHeader } from '../components/ui/profile/CompleteProfileHeader';
import { LgpdAgreement } from '../components/ui/profile/LgpdAgreement';

const safeJsonParse = <T,>(jsonString: string): T | null => {
  try {
    return jsonString ? JSON.parse(jsonString) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

export function CompleteProfilePage() {
  const { user, signOut, setProfileComplete, setUserData } = useAuthStore();
  const { iesList, campusList, cursoList, loadingIes, loadingCampus, loadingCursos, fetchIesList, fetchCampusList, fetchCursoList, clearCampusList, clearCursoList } = useMecStore();

  const [isDocModalOpen, setIsDocModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [docType, setDocType] = useState<DocumentType>('privacidade');

  const openDocument = (type: DocumentType) => {
    setDocType(type);
    setIsDocModalOpen(true);
  };

  const [nome, setNome] = useState('');
  const [cpf, setCpf] = useState('');
  const [celular, setCelular] = useState('');
  const [uf, setUf] = useState('');
  const [registroCRA, setRegistroCRA] = useState('');
  const [comoSoube, setComoSoube] = useState('');
  const [formacao, setFormacao] = useState('');
  const [areaAtuacao, setAreaAtuacao] = useState('');
  const [semestre, setSemestre] = useState('');

  const [ies, setIes] = useState('');
  const [campus, setCampus] = useState('');
  const [curso, setCurso] = useState('');
  const [cadastroManualIes, setCadastroManualIes] = useState(false);

  const [lgpd, setLgpd] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [salvando, setSalvando] = useState(false);
  const [mensagemSucesso, setMensagemSucesso] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const isEstudante = formacao.startsWith('Estudante de Administração');
    if (isEstudante && iesList.length === 0) {
      fetchIesList();
    }
  }, [formacao, iesList.length, fetchIesList]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ies) {
      const selectedIes = safeJsonParse<{ codIes: string; nomeIes: string }>(ies);
      if (selectedIes) fetchCampusList(selectedIes.codIes);
    } else {
      clearCampusList();
    }
    setCampus('');
    setCurso('');
  }, [ies, fetchCampusList, clearCampusList]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (campus && ies) {
      const selectedIes = safeJsonParse<{ codIes: string; nomeIes: string }>(ies);
      const selectedCampus = safeJsonParse<{ codLocal: string; nomeLocal: string }>(campus);

      if (selectedIes && selectedCampus) {
        let filtroGrau = '';
        if (formacao.includes('Bacharelado')) filtroGrau = 'Bacharelado';
        if (formacao.includes('Tecnólogo')) filtroGrau = 'Tecnológico';
        fetchCursoList(selectedIes.codIes, selectedCampus.codLocal, filtroGrau);
      }
    } else {
      clearCursoList();
    }
    setCurso('');
  }, [campus, ies, formacao, fetchCursoList, clearCursoList]);

  const isOutraArea = formacao === 'Profissional de outra área' || formacao === 'Estudante de outra área';
  const isEstudanteAdm = formacao.startsWith('Estudante de Administração');
  const isTecnologo = formacao === 'Estudante de Administração - Curso Tecnólogo';
  const semestresDisponiveis = isTecnologo ? 6 : 10;

  const handleSalvarDados = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    setSalvando(true);
    setMensagemSucesso('');
    setError('');

    if (nome.trim().length < 2) {
      setError('Informe seu nome completo.');
      setSalvando(false);
      return;
    }

    if (!validateCPF(cpf)) {
      setError('CPF inválido. Verifique o número digitado.');
      setSalvando(false);
      return;
    }

    if (isOutraArea && !areaAtuacao) {
      setError('Selecione a sua área de atuação ou estudo.');
      setSalvando(false);
      return;
    }

    let finalIes = ies ? ies.trim() : null;
    let finalCampus = campus ? campus.trim() : null;
    let finalCurso = curso ? curso.trim() : null;

    if (isEstudanteAdm && !cadastroManualIes) {
      const selectedIes = safeJsonParse<{ nomeIes: string }>(ies);
      const selectedCampus = safeJsonParse<{ nomeLocal: string }>(campus);
      const selectedCurso = safeJsonParse<{ nomeCurso: string }>(curso);
      finalIes = selectedIes?.nomeIes?.trim() || null;
      finalCampus = selectedCampus?.nomeLocal?.trim() || null;
      finalCurso = selectedCurso?.nomeCurso?.trim() || null;
    }

    if (isEstudanteAdm) {
      if (!finalIes || finalIes.length < (cadastroManualIes ? 3 : 2)) {
        setError('Selecione ou informe a sua Instituição de Ensino.');
        setSalvando(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!finalCampus || finalCampus.length < 2) {
        setError('Selecione ou informe o seu Campus ou Polo.');
        setSalvando(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!finalCurso || finalCurso.length < (cadastroManualIes ? 3 : 2)) {
        setError('Selecione ou informe o seu Curso.');
        setSalvando(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!semestre) {
        setError('Selecione em qual semestre você está.');
        setSalvando(false);
        return;
      }
    }

    if (lgpd !== true) {
      setError('É obrigatório aceitar os termos da LGPD para concluir o cadastro. O aceite é requisito necessário para acesso aos serviços do CRA-SP Educa.');
      setSalvando(false);
      return;
    }

    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    try {
      const payload = { 
        nome: nome.trim(), cpf, celular, uf, 
        registroCRA: registroCRA as 'Sim' | 'Não', 
        comoSoube, formacao, 
        areaAtuacao: isOutraArea ? (areaAtuacao || null) : null, 
        semestre: isEstudanteAdm ? (semestre || null) : null, 
        ies: isEstudanteAdm ? finalIes : null, 
        campus: isEstudanteAdm ? finalCampus : null, 
        curso: isEstudanteAdm ? finalCurso : null,
        cadastroManualIes: isEstudanteAdm ? cadastroManualIes : false,
        lgpd: true
      };

      await api.usuarios.salvarPerfil(payload as any);
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;

      setMensagemSucesso('Dados salvos com sucesso!');
      setUserData({ ...payload, jornadaCursos: [], jornadaVagas: [] });
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
          setProfileComplete(true);
        }
      }, 1000);

    } catch (err: any) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      setError(err.message || 'Erro de conexão ao salvar.');
    } finally {
      if (isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) {
        setSalvando(false);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col font-sans">
      <CompleteProfileHeader onSignOut={signOut} />

      <main className="flex-1 flex justify-center py-12 px-[16px] md:px-8">
        <div className="w-full max-w-4xl">
          <div className="mb-10 text-center">
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 tracking-tight mb-[12px]">
              Complete seu cadastro
            </h2>
            <p className="text-gray-600 text-lg max-w-2xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
              Só precisamos de mais alguns dados para personalizar sua experiência.
            </p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSalvarDados} className="flex flex-col gap-8 bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[16px] shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
            <ProfileFormFields
              nome={nome} setNome={setNome}
              email={user?.email || ''}
              cpf={cpf} setCpf={setCpf}
              celular={celular} setCelular={setCelular}
              uf={uf} setUf={setUf}
              registroCRA={registroCRA} setRegistroCRA={setRegistroCRA}
              comoSoube={comoSoube} setComoSoube={setComoSoube}
              formacao={formacao} setFormacao={setFormacao}
              areaAtuacao={areaAtuacao} setAreaAtuacao={setAreaAtuacao}
              ies={ies} setIes={setIes}
              campus={campus} setCampus={setCampus}
              curso={curso} setCurso={setCurso}
              semestre={semestre} setSemestre={setSemestre}
              iesList={iesList} campusList={campusList} cursoList={cursoList}
              loadingIes={loadingIes} loadingCampus={loadingCampus} loadingCursos={loadingCursos}
              isOutraArea={isOutraArea} isEstudanteAdm={isEstudanteAdm}
              semestresDisponiveis={semestresDisponiveis}
              cadastroManualIes={cadastroManualIes} setCadastroManualIes={setCadastroManualIes}
            />

            <LgpdAgreement 
              lgpd={lgpd} 
              setLgpd={setLgpd} 
              onOpenDocument={openDocument} 
            />

            {error && (
              <div role="alert" className="bg-red-50 text-red-700 px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl border border-red-200 text-sm font-semibold mt-2">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            {mensagemSucesso && (
              <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="bg-green-50 text-green-700 px-[16px] py-[12px] rounded-xl border border-green-200 text-sm font-semibold mt-2 flex items-center gap-[8px]">
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
                  <path d="M22 11.08V12C21.9988 14.1564 21.3001 16.2547 20.0093 17.9818C18.7185 19.709 16.9033 20.9725 14.8354 21.5839C12.7674 22.1953 10.5573 22.1219 8.53447 21.3746C6.51168 20.6273 4.78465 19.2461 3.61096 17.4371C2.43727 15.628 1.87979 13.4881 2.02168 11.3363C2.16356 9.18455 2.99721 7.13631 4.39828 5.49706C5.79935 3.85781 7.69279 2.71537 9.79619 2.24013C11.8996 1.7649 14.0976 1.98232 16.06 2.85999M22 4L12 14.01L9 11.01" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
                </svg>
                {mensagemSucesso}
              </div>
            )}

            <button 
              type="submit" 
              disabled={salvando}
              aria-label="Salvar dados e acessar a plataforma"
              className="bg-blue-900 text-white px-[16px] py-[16px] w-full mt-4 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 hover:bg-blue-800 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-900/20 font-bold uppercase text-sm tracking-wide disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(30,58,138,0.2)] hover:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(30,58,138,0.4)]"
            >
              {salvando ? 'Salvando Dados...' : 'Salvar e Acessar Plataforma'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </main>

      <DocumentModal 
        isOpen={isDocModalOpen}
        initialDocument={docType}
        onClose={() => setIsDocModalOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import { VitrineCard } from "../components/showcase/VitrineCard";

export function ExplorarCursos() {

            {filters.cursosFiltrados.length}{" "}
            {filters.cursosFiltrados.length === 1
              ? "RESULTADO ENCONTRADO"
              : "RESULTADOS ENCONTRADOS"}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
          {loading && filters.cursosFiltrados.length === 0 ? (
            Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (

                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-5/6"></div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <div className="h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full w-24"></div>
                  <div className="h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full w-24"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            filters.cursosFiltrados.map((curso, index) => (
              <VitrineCard
                key={curso.id || index}
                curso={curso}
                isInscrito={jornadaCursos.some(
                  (j) => j.cursoId === curso.id && j.status !== "DESISTIU",

        </div>

        {filters.cursosFiltrados.length === 0 && !loading && (
          <div className="w-full py-16 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center bg-gray-50 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 mt-8 rounded-[16px]">
            <SearchX className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-400 mb-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="uppercase tracking-widest text-sm font-bold text-gray-500">
              Nenhum curso atende aos filtros aplicados.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {cursoDetalhe && (
        <CursoDetalheModal
          cursoDetalhe={cursoDetalhe}
          isInscrito={jornadaCursos.some(
            (j) => j.cursoId === cursoDetalhe.id && j.status !== "DESISTIU",
          )}
          loadingMatricula={loadingCursoId === cursoDetalhe.id}
          onClose={() => setCursoDetalhe(null)}
          onPreMatricula={handlePreMatricula}
        />
      )}

      {cursoMatricula && (
        <PreMatriculaCursoModal
          cursoMatricula={cursoMatricula}
          onClose={() => setCursoMatricula(null)}
          onOpenAva={() => setWaitingReturnForCursoId(cursoMatricula.id)}
        />
      )}

      {returnModalConfig && (
        <ConfirmationModal
          isOpen={returnModalConfig.isOpen}
          variant="success"
          title="Matrícula Efetuada?"
          description="Bem-vindo de volta! E aí, conseguiu realizar a matrícula no Ambiente da Instituição Participante?"
          cancelText="Ainda não"
          confirmText="Sim, me matriculei!"
          onCancel={handleCancelReturn}
          onConfirm={() => handleConfirmReturn(returnModalConfig.jornadaId)}
        />
      )}

      {errorMessage && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 px-6 py-[16px] rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-start gap-4 z-50 max-w-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-5"
        >
          <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-red-600 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm">Ops, ocorreu um erro</h4>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1 opacity-90">{errorMessage}</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Fechar notificação de erro"
            onClick={() => setErrorMessage(null)}
            className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 transition-colors ml-auto"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/ExplorarCursos.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/ExplorarVagas.tsx ---
import { VagaCard } from "../components/showcase/VagaCard";
import { VagaDetalheModal } from "../components/ui/VagaDetalheModal";
import { VagasFilterBar } from "../components/showcase/VagasFilterBar";
import { InteresseVagaModal } from "../components/ui/InteresseVagaModal";
import { useVagasFilters } from "../hooks/useVagasFilters";
import { useVagasInteresse } from "../hooks/useVagasInteresse";
import { ConfirmationModal } from "../components/ui/ConfirmationModal";
import { SearchX, AlertCircle, X } from "lucide-react";
import { VAGAS_COLORS } from "../lib/vagasImages";
import { useImagensStore } from "../store/useImagensStore";

export function ExplorarVagas() {
  const filters = useVagasFilters();
  const { imagensPublicas } = useImagensStore();
  const {
    loading,
    jornadaVagas,
    vagaDetalhe,
    setVagaDetalhe,
    vagaInteresse,
    setVagaInteresse,
    loadingVagaId,
    setWaitingReturnForVagaId,
    handleQueroConcorrer,
    returnModalConfig,
    handleConfirmReturn,
    handleCancelReturn,
    errorMessage,
    setErrorMessage
  } = useVagasInteresse();

  return (
    <div className="w-full relative flex flex-col gap-12">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
        <VagasFilterBar
          filtroEmpresa={filters.filtroEmpresa}
          setFiltroEmpresa={filters.setFiltroEmpresa}
          empresas={filters.empresas}
          filtroTipoVaga={filters.filtroTipoVaga}
          setFiltroTipoVaga={filters.setFiltroTipoVaga}
          tiposDeVaga={filters.tiposDeVaga}
          filtroCidade={filters.filtroCidade}
          setFiltroCidade={filters.setFiltroCidade}
          cidades={filters.cidades}
          filtroBolsa={filters.filtroBolsa}
          setFiltroBolsa={filters.setFiltroBolsa}
          bolsasDisponiveis={filters.bolsasDisponiveis}
        />

        <div className="flex items-center">
          <span className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 rounded-full px-[16px] py-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest border border-gray-200">
            {filters.vagasFiltradas.length}{" "}
            {filters.vagasFiltradas.length === 1
              ? "RESULTADO ENCONTRADO"
              : "RESULTADOS ENCONTRADOS"}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
          {loading && filters.vagasFiltradas.length === 0 ? (
            Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="bg-white border border-[#f3f4f6] rounded-[16px] p-6 shadow-sm animate-pulse h-[300px] flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                  <div className="h-6 bg-gray-200 rounded w-3/4"></div>
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-full"></div>
                  <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-5/6"></div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <div className="h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full w-24"></div>
                  <div className="h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full w-24"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            filters.vagasFiltradas.map((vaga, index) => {
              const displayColor = VAGAS_COLORS[index % VAGAS_COLORS.length];
              const hasImages = imagensPublicas && imagensPublicas.length > 0;
              const displayImage = hasImages ? imagensPublicas[index % imagensPublicas.length] : 'data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7';
              return (
                <VagaCard
                  key={vaga.codigoDaVaga || index}
                  vaga={vaga}
                  isCandidatado={jornadaVagas.some(
                    (c) => c.vagaId === vaga.id && c.status !== "DESISTIU",
                  )}
                  loadingJornadaVaga={loadingVagaId === vaga.id}
                  onDetalhe={setVagaDetalhe}
                  onQueroConcorrer={handleQueroConcorrer}
                  parseBolsa={filters.parseBolsa}
                  displayColor={displayColor}
                  displayImage={displayImage}
                />
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {filters.vagasFiltradas.length === 0 && !loading && (
          <div className="w-full py-16 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center bg-gray-50 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 mt-8 rounded-[16px]">
            <SearchX className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-400 mb-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="uppercase tracking-widest text-sm font-bold text-gray-500">
              Nenhuma vaga atende aos filtros aplicados.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {vagaDetalhe && (
        <VagaDetalheModal
          vagaDetalhe={vagaDetalhe}
          isCandidatado={jornadaVagas.some(
            (c) => c.vagaId === vagaDetalhe.id && c.status !== "DESISTIU",
          )}
          loadingJornadaVaga={loadingVagaId === vagaDetalhe.id}
          onClose={() => setVagaDetalhe(null)}
          onQueroConcorrer={handleQueroConcorrer}
        />
      )}

      {vagaInteresse && (
        <InteresseVagaModal
          vagaInteresse={vagaInteresse}
          onClose={() => setVagaInteresse(null)}
          onOpenPortal={() => setWaitingReturnForVagaId(vagaInteresse.id)}
        />
      )}

      {returnModalConfig && (
        <ConfirmationModal
          isOpen={returnModalConfig.isOpen}
          variant="success"
          title="Jornada Realizada?"
          description="Bem-vindo de volta! Conseguiu finalizar sua inscrição no portal da vaga?"
          cancelText="Ainda não"
          confirmText="Sim, já estou participando!"
          onCancel={handleCancelReturn}
          onConfirm={() => handleConfirmReturn(returnModalConfig.jornadaVagaId)}
        />
      )}

      {errorMessage && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 px-6 py-[16px] rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-start gap-4 z-50 max-w-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-5"
        >
          <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-red-600 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm">Ops, ocorreu um erro</h4>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1 opacity-90">{errorMessage}</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Fechar notificação de erro"
            onClick={() => setErrorMessage(null)}
            className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 transition-colors ml-auto"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/JornadaCursosInicio.tsx ---
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../api";
import {
  useAuthStore,
  AuthSessionSnapshot,
  getAuthSessionSnapshot,
  isAuthSessionCurrent,
} from "../store/useAuthStore";
import { useJornadaCursosStore, JornadaCurso } from "../store/useJornadaCursosStore";
import { useJornadaVagasStore } from "../store/useJornadaVagasStore";
import { ChevronRight, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigationStore } from "../store/useNavigationStore";
import { ListaJornadaCursos } from "../components/showcase/ListaJornadaCursos";
import { ListaJornadaVagas } from "../components/showcase/ListaJornadaVagas";
import { AcessoRapido } from "../components/showcase/AcessoRapido";
import { UploadCertificadoModal } from "../components/ui/UploadCertificadoModal";
import { JornadaCursosKpis } from "../components/showcase/JornadaCursosKpis";
import { CarteiraEstudanteHero } from "../components/showcase/CarteiraEstudanteHero";

export function JornadaCursosInicio() {
  const { user, userData } = useAuthStore();
  const setActiveTab = useNavigationStore((s) => s.setActiveTab);
  const jornadaCursos = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.jornadaCursos);
  const updateJornadaCursoLocal = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.updateJornadaCursoLocal);
  const jornadaVagas = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.jornadaVagas);
  const updateJornadaVagaLocal = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.updateJornadaVagaLocal);

  const [evaluatingJornadaCurso, setEvaluatingJornadaCurso] = useState<JornadaCurso | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [uploadSuccess, setUploadSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dismissedCarteiraHero, setDismissedCarteiraHero] = useState(false);
  const evaluationOpIdRef = useRef(0);

  const handleSetEvaluatingJornada = (jornada: JornadaCurso | null) => {
    evaluationOpIdRef.current += 1;
    setEvaluatingJornadaCurso(jornada);
  };

  const handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus = async (
    vagaId: string,
    newStatus: string,
  ) => {
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    const previous = jornadaVagas.find((c) => c.vagaId === vagaId);
    const previousStatus = previous?.status;

    updateJornadaVagaLocal(vagaId, newStatus as any);

    try {
      await api.jornadaVagas.atualizarStatus(vagaId, newStatus);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      console.error(error);
      if (previousStatus) updateJornadaVagaLocal(vagaId, previousStatus as any);
      setErrorMessage("Falha de conexão. A ação foi revertida para sua segurança.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (cursoId: string, newStatus: string) => {
    const sessionSnap = getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    const previousJornadaCurso = jornadaCursos.find((j) => j.cursoId === cursoId);
    const previousStatus = previousJornadaCurso?.status;

    updateJornadaCursoLocal(cursoId, newStatus as any);

    try {
      await api.jornadaCursos.atualizarStatus(cursoId, newStatus);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;
      console.error(error);
      if (previousStatus) updateJornadaCursoLocal(cursoId, previousStatus as any);
      setErrorMessage("Falha de conexão. A ação foi revertida para sua segurança.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitEvaluation = async (
    file: File | null,
    evalData: { notaCurso?: number; notaPlataforma?: number; notaMaterial?: number; comentario?: string },
    originSession?: AuthSessionSnapshot,
    onFileUploaded?: () => void,
  ) => {
    if (!evaluatingJornadaCurso) return;
    const sessionSnap = originSession || getAuthSessionSnapshot();
    if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap)) return;

    const currentOpId = ++evaluationOpIdRef.current;
    const targetCursoId = evaluatingJornadaCurso.cursoId;
    const targetStatus = evaluatingJornadaCurso.status;

    try {
      let url = evaluatingJornadaCurso.certificadoUrl;
      if (file) {
        url = await api.certificados.enviar(targetCursoId, file);
        if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap) || evaluationOpIdRef.current !== currentOpId) {
          return;
        }
        updateJornadaCursoLocal(targetCursoId, targetStatus, {
          certificadoUrl: url,
          avaliacao: evalData,
        });
        setEvaluatingJornadaCurso((prev) =>
          prev && prev.cursoId === targetCursoId
            ? { ...prev, certificadoUrl: url, avaliacao: evalData }
            : prev
        );
        onFileUploaded?.();
      }

      if (!url) {
        throw new Error("Selecione um certificado válido antes de enviar.");
      }

      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap) || evaluationOpIdRef.current !== currentOpId) {
        return;
      }

      await api.jornadaCursos.atualizarStatus(targetCursoId, "EM_ANALISE", {
        certificadoUrl: url,
        avaliacao: evalData,
      });
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap) || evaluationOpIdRef.current !== currentOpId) {
        return;
      }

      updateJornadaCursoLocal(targetCursoId, "EM_ANALISE", {
        certificadoUrl: url,
        avaliacao: evalData,
      });
      setEvaluatingJornadaCurso((prev) => (prev && prev.cursoId === targetCursoId ? null : prev));
      setUploadSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setUploadSuccess(false), 5000);
    } catch (error: any) {
      if (!isAuthSessionCurrent(sessionSnap) || evaluationOpIdRef.current !== currentOpId) {
        return;
      }
      console.error(error);
      setErrorMessage(error.message || "Erro ao enviar o certificado.");
      setTimeout(() => setErrorMessage(null), 5000);
      throw error;
    }
  };

  const firstName = userData?.nome?.split(" ")[0] || user?.displayName?.split(" ")[0] || "Profissional";
  
  const showCarteiraHero = userData?.formacao?.startsWith('Estudante de Administração') 
                           && userData?.uf === 'SP' 
                           && userData?.statusCarteira !== 'JA_TENHO' 
                           && !dismissedCarteiraHero;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-12">
      {showCarteiraHero ? (
        <CarteiraEstudanteHero 
          firstName={firstName} 
          onDismissSession={() => setDismissedCarteiraHero(true)} 
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] bg-gradient-to-r from-[#1e3a8a] to-[#1e40af] p-[24px] sm:p-[32px] lg:p-[40px] shadow-[0_10px_15px_-3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1),_0_4px_6px_-4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-[24px] justify-between">
          <div 
            className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 rounded-full bg-white/5"
            style={{ transform: 'translateY(-50%) translateX(33.333333%)' }}
          />
          <div 
            className="absolute bottom-0 right-32 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-white/5"
            style={{ transform: 'translateY(33.333333%) translateX(25%)' }}
          />

          <div className="relative z-10 w-full flex flex-col justify-center">
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl lg:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-2">
              Olá, {firstName}!
            </h1>
            <p className="text-[16px] lg:text-lg text-[#dbeafe] leading-relaxed max-w-2xl mb-6">
              Mantenha o status das suas matrículas, certificados e processos seletivos atualizado para receber recomendações de cursos e vagas sob medida para o seu momento.
            </p>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-[12px]">
              <button 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('cursos')}
                className="group inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] w-full sm:w-auto rounded-full bg-yellow-500 border border-transparent text-black py-2.5 px-6 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-yellow-400"
              >
                Explorar Cursos
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" aria-hidden="true" />
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('vagas')}
                className="group inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] w-full sm:w-auto rounded-full bg-transparent border border-white/40 text-white py-2.5 px-6 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-white/10 hover:border-white/60"
              >
                Explorar Vagas
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" aria-hidden="true" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <JornadaCursosKpis jornadaCursos={jornadaCursos} />

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-12 w-full">
        <ListaJornadaCursos
          jornadaCursos={jornadaCursos}
          handleUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
          setEvaluatingJornada={handleSetEvaluatingJornada}
        />
        
        {jornadaVagas && jornadaVagas.length > 0 && (
          <ListaJornadaVagas
            jornadaVagas={jornadaVagas}
            handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus={handleUpdateJornadaVagaStatus}
          />
        )}

        <AcessoRapido />
      </div>

      {evaluatingJornadaCurso && (
        <UploadCertificadoModal
          onClose={() => handleSetEvaluatingJornada(null)}
          existingCertificadoUrl={evaluatingJornadaCurso.certificadoUrl}
          existingAvaliacao={evaluatingJornadaCurso.avaliacao}
          onSubmit={handleSubmitEvaluation}
        />
      )}

      {uploadSuccess && (
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 text-green-800 px-6 py-[16px] rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-start gap-4 z-50 max-w-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-5"
        >
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-green-600 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm">Certificado Enviado</h4>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1 opacity-90">Recebemos seu certificado com sucesso! O CRA-SP fará a análise em breve.</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Fechar notificação de sucesso"
            onClick={() => setUploadSuccess(false)}
            className="text-green-600 hover:text-green-800 transition-colors ml-auto"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {errorMessage && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 px-6 py-[16px] rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-start gap-4 z-50 max-w-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-5"
        >
          <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-red-600 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm">Ops, ocorreu um erro</h4>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1 opacity-90">{errorMessage}</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Fechar notificação de erro"
            onClick={() => setErrorMessage(null)}
            className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 transition-colors ml-auto"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-[20px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/JornadaCursosInicio.tsx ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/LoggedDashboard.tsx ---
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigationStore } from '../store/useNavigationStore';
import { ExplorarCursos } from './ExplorarCursos';
import { ExplorarVagas } from './ExplorarVagas';
import { JornadaCursosInicio } from './JornadaCursosInicio';
import { MinhasConquistas } from './MinhasConquistas';

export function LoggedDashboard() {
  const { activeTab } = useNavigationStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
  }, [activeTab]);

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#f9fafb] text-gray-900 min-h-screen">
      <div className="w-full max-w-[1152px] mx-auto px-[16px] py-8">
        {activeTab === 'inicio' && <JornadaCursosInicio />}

        {activeTab === 'dados' && (
          <div className="border border-black p-12 text-center mt-4 rounded-xl">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black uppercase mb-4 tracking-tight">Meus Dados</h2>
            <p className="uppercase tracking-widest text-sm font-bold">Gestão de perfil estará disponível em breve.</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'cursos' && <ExplorarCursos />}

        {activeTab === 'vagas' && <ExplorarVagas />}

        {activeTab === 'conquistas' && <MinhasConquistas />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/LoginPage.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Mail, Lock, Presentation } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuthStore } from '../store/useAuthStore';
import { useNavigationStore } from '../store/useNavigationStore';
import { signInWithEmailAndPassword, signInWithPopup, createUserWithEmailAndPassword, sendPasswordResetEmail } from 'firebase/auth';
import { auth, googleProvider, microsoftProvider } from '../lib/firebase';
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from 'motion/react';

export function LoginPage() {
  const { setIsLoginOpen } = useAuthStore();
  const setActiveTab = useNavigationStore(state => state.setActiveTab);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isRegisterMode, setIsRegisterMode] = useState(false);
  const shouldReduceMotion = useReducedMotion();

  const handleForgotPassword = async () => {
    if (!email) {
      setError('Digite seu e-mail no campo acima para receber as instruções de redefinição.');
      return;
    }
    try {
      await sendPasswordResetEmail(auth, email);
      setSuccessMsg('E-mail de redefinição enviado com sucesso! Verifique sua caixa de entrada.');
      setError('');
    } catch (_err) {
      setError('Erro ao enviar e-mail de redefinição de senha. Verifique o endereço informado.');
    }
  };


  const handleAuth = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (isRegisterMode) {
        await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      } else {
        await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      }
      setActiveTab('inicio');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      if (err.code === 'auth/email-already-in-use') {
        setError('Este e-mail já está em uso.');
      } else if (err.code === 'auth/weak-password') {
        setError('A senha deve ter pelo menos 6 caracteres.');
      } else {
        setError(isRegisterMode ? 'Erro ao criar conta.' : 'Credenciais inválidas ou erro de conexão.');
      }
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGoogleLogin = async () => {
    try {
      await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
      setActiveTab('inicio');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Falha ao autenticar com Google.');
    }
  };

  const handleMicrosoftLogin = async () => {
    try {
      await signInWithPopup(auth, microsoftProvider);
      setActiveTab('inicio');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Falha ao autenticar com Microsoft.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="h-screen flex flex-col lg:flex-row bg-white font-sans overflow-hidden">

        <div className="hidden lg:block lg:w-1/2 relative bg-[#1e3a8a] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
            <div className="absolute top-[-20%] left-[-10%] w-[70%] h-[70%] bg-blue-600 rounded-full opacity-30 blur-[100px]"></div>
            <div className="absolute bottom-[-20%] right-[-10%] w-[60%] h-[60%] bg-blue-900 rounded-full opacity-40 blur-[120px]"></div>
          </div>

          <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col h-full justify-center p-12 lg:p-16 xl:p-24">
            <div className="w-full max-w-lg mx-auto">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-[16px] backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20 mb-8 shadow-lg">
                <Presentation className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
              </div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl xl:text-5xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white mb-6 leading-tight">
                Bem-vindo ao<br />
                <span className="text-[#facc15]">CRA-SP Educa</span>
              </h1>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full lg:w-1/2 flex flex-col relative [.alto-contraste_&]:border-l [.alto-contraste_&]:border-white px-8 sm:px-12 lg:px-16 xl:px-24 bg-white h-screen lg:h-full overflow-y-auto">

          <button 
            onClick={() => setIsLoginOpen(false)} 
            className="absolute top-6 left-6 sm:top-8 sm:left-8 flex items-center gap-[8px] text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition-colors font-medium px-3 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-100 text-sm z-10"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Voltar</span>
          </button>

          <div className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto flex flex-col min-h-full pt-[15vh] pb-8">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: shouldReduceMotion ? 0 : 50 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: "easeOut" }}
              className="flex flex-col gap-6"
            >
              <div className="mb-2 text-left">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-gray-900 tracking-tight">{isRegisterMode ? "Crie sua conta" : "Acesse sua conta"}</h2>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleAuth} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
                  E-mail
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none z-10 bg-transparent hc-yellow">
                    <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </div>
                  <input id="email" name="email"
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="seu@email.com"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="pl-10 border border-gray-300 text-gray-900 px-3 py-2 w-full outline-none rounded-lg focus:border-[#1e3a8a] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#1e3a8a] transition-all bg-white text-sm shadow-sm"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
                  Senha
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none z-10 bg-transparent hc-yellow">
                    <Lock className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </div>
                  <input id="password" name="password"
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="pl-10 border border-gray-300 text-gray-900 px-3 py-2 w-full outline-none rounded-lg focus:border-[#1e3a8a] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#1e3a8a] transition-all bg-white text-sm shadow-sm"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                {!isRegisterMode && (
                  <div className="flex justify-start mt-2">
                    <button 
                      type="button" 
                      onClick={handleForgotPassword} 
                      className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900"
                    >
                      Esqueceu a senha?
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div role="alert" className="bg-red-50 text-red-700 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-red-200 text-sm font-medium">
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}
              
              {successMsg && (
                <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="bg-green-50 text-green-700 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-green-200 text-sm font-medium">
                  {successMsg}
                </div>
              )}

              <button 
                type="submit" 
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white px-[16px] py-2.5 w-full mt-4 rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-blue-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-900 focus:ring-offset-1 font-medium text-sm disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-sm hc-border"
              >
                {isSubmitting ? 'Aguarde...' : (isRegisterMode ? 'Criar Conta' : 'Entrar')}
              </button>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 my-2">
                <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-gray-200"></div>
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Ou</span>
                <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-gray-200"></div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                <button 
                  type="button" 
                  onClick={handleGoogleLogin} 
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-[16px] py-2 w-full rounded-lg transition-colors hover:bg-gray-50 font-medium text-sm shadow-sm hc-border"
                >
                  <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
                    <path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4"/>
                    <path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853"/>
                    <path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z" fill="#FBBC05"/>
                    <path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z" fill="#EA4335"/>
                  </svg>
                  Continuar com Google
                </button>

                <button 
                  type="button" 
                  onClick={handleMicrosoftLogin} 
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-[16px] py-2 w-full rounded-lg transition-colors hover:bg-gray-50 font-medium text-sm shadow-sm hc-border"
                >
                  <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 21 21" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
                    <path d="M10 0H0V10H10V0Z" fill="#F25022"/>
                    <path d="M21 0H11V10H21V0Z" fill="#7FBA00"/>
                    <path d="M10 11H0V21H10V11Z" fill="#00A4EF"/>
                    <path d="M21 11H11V21H21V11Z" fill="#FFB900"/>
                  </svg>
                  Continuar com Microsoft
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
            </motion.div>

            <div className="mt-8 text-center">
              {isRegisterMode ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                  Já tem uma conta? <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsRegisterMode(false)} className="text-[#1e3a8a] font-medium hover:underline">Faça login</button>
                </p>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                  Não tem uma conta? <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsRegisterMode(true)} className="text-[#1e3a8a] font-medium hover:underline">Crie sua conta</button>
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
    </>
  );
}

import { useAuthStore } from "../store/useAuthStore";
import { useJornadaCursosStore } from "../store/useJornadaCursosStore";
import { useJornadaVagasStore } from "../store/useJornadaVagasStore";


import { Award, BookOpen, Briefcase, Star, Zap, Shield, Crown, CheckCircle2, Calendar, Clock } from "lucide-react";
const formatDate = (dateObj: any) => {
  if (!dateObj) return "Data Indisponível";
  try {
    const d = dateObj.toDate ? dateObj.toDate() : new Date(dateObj);
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR').format(d);
  } catch {
    return "Data Indisponível";
  }
};
export function MinhasConquistas() {
  const { user, userData } = useAuthStore();
  const jornadaCursos = useJornadaCursosStore((s) => s.jornadaCursos);
  const jornadaVagas = useJornadaVagasStore((s) => s.jornadaVagas);
  const conquistasCursos = jornadaCursos.filter((j) => j.status === "CONCLUIDO");
  const conquistasVagas = jornadaVagas.filter((j) => j.status === "CONTRATADO");
  const totalConquistas = conquistasCursos.length + conquistasVagas.length;
  const firstName = userData?.nome?.split(" ")[0] || user?.displayName?.split(" ")[0] || "Profissional";
  const horasCertificadas = conquistasCursos.reduce((acc, j) => {
    const cargaHoraria = (j as any).cursoSnapshot?.cargaHoraria;
    if (cargaHoraria) {
      const match = String(cargaHoraria).match(/\d+/);
      if (match) return acc + parseInt(match[0], 10);
    }
    return acc;
  }, 0);
  const badges = [
    {
      id: "iniciativa",
      nome: "Selo Iniciativa",
      descricao: "10h em cursos",
      target: 10,
      icon: Zap,
      color: "bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 border-orange-200",
      active: horasCertificadas >= 10,
    },
    {
      id: "dedicacao",
      nome: "Selo Dedicação",
      descricao: "50h em cursos",
      target: 50,
      icon: Shield,
      color: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-600 border-blue-200",
      active: horasCertificadas >= 50,
    },
    {
      id: "destaque",
      nome: "Selo Destaque",
      descricao: "100h em cursos",
      target: 100,
      icon: Star,
      color: "bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 border-cyan-200",
      active: horasCertificadas >= 100,
    },
    {
      id: "protagonista",
      nome: "Selo Protagonista",
      descricao: "250+h em cursos",
      target: 250,
      icon: Crown,
      color: "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-600 border-yellow-300",
      active: horasCertificadas >= 250,
    }
  ];
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-12 w-full">
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-600 to-yellow-500 p-[24px] sm:p-[32px] shadow-[0_10px_15px_-3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1),_0_4px_6px_-4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-[24px] justify-between lg:h-[200px]">
        <div 
          className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 rounded-full bg-white/10"
          style={{ transform: 'translateY(-50%) translateX(33.333333%)' }}
        />
        <div 
          className="absolute bottom-0 right-32 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-white/10"
          style={{ transform: 'translateY(33.333333%) translateX(25%)' }}
        />
        <div className="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-[24px]">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 backdrop-blur-sm border border-white/30">
            <Award className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl lg:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight text-white mb-2">
              Suas Conquistas, {firstName}!
            </h1>
            <p className="text-[16px] lg:text-lg text-yellow-50 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
              Acompanhe seu progresso, celebre os certificados validados e colecione os selos da sua jornada.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20 rounded-2xl px-[24px] py-[16px] min-w-[140px]">
          <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-yellow-50 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Horas Certificadas</span>
          <span className="text-3xl font-black text-white">{horasCertificadas}h</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-[16px]">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827] flex items-center gap-2">
          <Award className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
          Medalhas Desbloqueadas
        </h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-[16px]">
          {badges.map((badge) => {
            const Icon = badge.icon;
            return (
              <div 
                key={badge.id}
                className={`relative overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] border p-[20px] flex flex-col items-center text-center transition-all duration-300 ${
                  badge.active 
                    ? `bg-white shadow-sm border-gray-200` 
                    : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 opacity-60 grayscale'
                }`}
              >
                <div className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4 ${badge.active ? badge.color : 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 border-gray-300'} border`}>
                  <Icon className="w-7 h-7" />
                </div>
                <h3 className={`font-bold text-[15px] mb-1 ${badge.active ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                  {badge.nome}
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[13px] text-gray-500 font-medium">
                  {badge.descricao}
                </span>
                {badge.active && (
                  <div className="absolute top-3 right-3">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-green-500" />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
      {totalConquistas === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-16 px-[16px] bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-[16px] mt-[16px]">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
            <Award className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-300" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">Nenhuma conquista ainda</h2>
          <p className="text-gray-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Quando você finalizar seus cursos e for aprovado nos processos seletivos, suas vitórias ficarão registradas aqui.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-[32px] mt-[16px]">
          {conquistasCursos.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-[16px]">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#e5e7eb] pb-2">
                <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
                  Cursos Concluídos
                </h2>
                <span className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[13px] ml-2">
                  {conquistasCursos.length}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="relative border-l-2 border-gray-100 ml-4 md:ml-[28px] mt-[8px] flex flex-col gap-[32px] pb-[16px]">
                {conquistasCursos.map((jornada) => {
                  const curso = jornada.cursoSnapshot || {};
                  const inst = curso.instituicao || curso.instituicaoCooperada || curso.nomeDaInstituicao || "Instituição Parceira";
                  return (
                    <div key={jornada.id} className="relative pl-[24px] md:pl-[32px]">
                      <span className="absolute -left-[21px] flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 ring-8 ring-[#f9fafb]">
                        <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600" />
                      </span>
                      <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-[16px] p-[20px] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-[16px]">
                          <div>
                            <h3 className="text-[17px] font-bold text-gray-900 leading-tight mb-1">{curso.titulo || curso.tituloEditado || curso.nome || curso.nomeDoCurso || "Curso não identificado"}</h3>
                            <p className="text-[14px] text-gray-500">{inst}</p>
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-[12px] shrink-0">
                            {curso.cargaHoraria && (
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-[6px] bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-[12px] py-[6px] rounded-full text-[13px] font-medium">
                                <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
                                {curso.cargaHoraria}
                              </div>
                            )}
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-[6px] bg-green-50 border border-green-100 text-green-700 px-[12px] py-[6px] rounded-full text-[13px] font-medium">
                              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-green-600" />
                              Concluído em {formatDate(jornada.updatedAt || jornada.createdAt)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {conquistasVagas.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-[16px]">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#e5e7eb] pb-2">
                <Briefcase className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#111827]">
                  Processos Aprovados
                </h2>
                <span className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[13px] ml-2">
                  {conquistasVagas.length}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="relative border-l-2 border-gray-100 ml-4 md:ml-[28px] mt-[8px] flex flex-col gap-[32px] pb-[16px]">
                {conquistasVagas.map((vagaJornada) => {
                  const vaga = vagaJornada.vagaSnapshot || {};
                  return (
                    <div key={vagaJornada.id} className="relative pl-[24px] md:pl-[32px]">
                      <span className="absolute -left-[21px] flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 ring-8 ring-[#f9fafb]">
                        <Briefcase className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600" />
                      </span>
                      <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-[16px] p-[20px] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-[16px]">
                          <div>
                            <h3 className="text-[17px] font-bold text-gray-900 leading-tight mb-1">{vaga.tipoDeVaga || vaga.titulo || vaga.nome || "Vaga não identificada"}</h3>
                            <p className="text-[14px] text-gray-500">{vaga.nomeDaEmpresa || vaga.empresa || "Empresa Parceira"}</p>
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-[12px] shrink-0">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-[6px] bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-[12px] py-[6px] rounded-full text-[13px] font-medium">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                              Contratado em {formatDate(vagaJornada.updatedAt || vagaJornada.createdAt)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/pages/PublicLandingPage.tsx ---
import { useEffect, Suspense, lazy } from 'react';
import { HeroBanner } from '../components/showcase/HeroBanner';
import { FeaturedCourses } from '../components/showcase/FeaturedCourses';
import { useHomeStore } from '../store/useHomeStore';
import { FadeIn } from '../components/ui/FadeIn';
import { LazyLoadSection } from '../components/ui/LazyLoadSection';

const FeaturedVagas = lazy(() => import('../components/showcase/FeaturedVagas').then(m => ({ default: m.FeaturedVagas })));
const HowItWorks = lazy(() => import('../components/showcase/HowItWorks').then(m => ({ default: m.HowItWorks })));
const SocialProof = lazy(() => import('../components/showcase/SocialProof').then(m => ({ default: m.SocialProof })));
const Institutions = lazy(() => import('../components/showcase/Institutions').then(m => ({ default: m.Institutions })));
const FaqAccordion = lazy(() => import('../components/showcase/FaqAccordion').then(m => ({ default: m.FaqAccordion })));

export function PublicLandingPage() {
  const { fetchHomeData } = useHomeStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchHomeData();
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
  }, [fetchHomeData]);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col flex-grow bg-white text-black min-h-[120vh] relative">
      <HeroBanner />
      
      <div id="cursos">
        <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
          <FeaturedCourses />
        </FadeIn>
      </div>

      <LazyLoadSection id="vagas" height="500px">
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-[500px] w-full bg-white animate-pulse" />}>
          <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
            <FeaturedVagas />
          </FadeIn>
        </Suspense>
      </LazyLoadSection>
      
      <LazyLoadSection id="jornada" height="400px">
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-[400px] w-full bg-gray-50 animate-pulse" />}>
          <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
            <HowItWorks />
          </FadeIn>
        </Suspense>
      </LazyLoadSection>
      
      <LazyLoadSection height="300px">
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-[300px] w-full bg-white animate-pulse" />}>
          <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
            <SocialProof />
          </FadeIn>
        </Suspense>
      </LazyLoadSection>
      
      <LazyLoadSection id="instituicoes" height="300px">
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-[300px] w-full bg-white animate-pulse" />}>
          <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
            <Institutions />
          </FadeIn>
        </Suspense>
      </LazyLoadSection>
      
      <LazyLoadSection id="perguntas" height="400px">
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-[400px] w-full bg-white animate-pulse" />}>
          <FadeIn delay={0.1}>
            <FaqAccordion />
          </FadeIn>
        </Suspense>
      </LazyLoadSection>
    </div>
  );
}

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useAccessibilityStore.ts ---
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { persist } from 'zustand/middleware';

interface AccessibilityState {
  fontSizeScale: number;
  isHighContrast: boolean;
  increaseFontSize: () => void;
  decreaseFontSize: () => void;
  resetFontSize: () => void;
  toggleHighContrast: () => void;
  resetAccessibility: () => void;
}

export const useAccessibilityStore = create<AccessibilityState>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      fontSizeScale: 1,
      isHighContrast: false,

      increaseFontSize: () =>
        set((state) => ({
          fontSizeScale: Math.min(state.fontSizeScale + 0.125, 1.5)
        })),

      decreaseFontSize: () =>
        set((state) => ({
          fontSizeScale: Math.max(state.fontSizeScale - 0.125, 1.0)
        })),

      resetFontSize: () =>
        set(() => ({
          fontSizeScale: 1
        })),

      toggleHighContrast: () =>
        set((state) => ({
          isHighContrast: !state.isHighContrast
        })),

      resetAccessibility: () =>
        set({
          fontSizeScale: 1,
          isHighContrast: false,
        }),
    }),
    {
      name: 'accessibility-storage',
    }
  )
);
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useAccessibilityStore.ts ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useAuthStore.ts ---
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { User, Unsubscribe } from 'firebase/auth';
import { useJornadaCursosStore } from './useJornadaCursosStore';
import { useJornadaVagasStore } from './useJornadaVagasStore';
import { useNavigationStore } from './useNavigationStore';

interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  profileComplete: boolean | null;
  userData: any | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isLoginOpen: boolean;
  totalUsuarios: number | null;
  totalMatriculas: number | null;
  totalDeCursos: number | null;
  totalDeVagas: number | null;
  setUser: (user: User | null) => void;
  setProfileComplete: (complete: boolean | null) => void;
  setUserData: (data: any | null) => void;
  setLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
  setIsLoginOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
  setTotalUsuarios: (total: number | null) => void;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  initializeAuth: () => void;
  cleanupAuth: () => void;
  fetchTotais: () => Promise<void>;
}

let globalUnsubscribe: Unsubscribe | null = null;
let isInitializingAuth = false;
let initGeneration = 0;
let authEpoch = 0;
let activeSessionUid: string | null = null;

export interface AuthSessionSnapshot {
  epoch: number;
  uid: string | null;
}

export function getAuthSessionSnapshot(): AuthSessionSnapshot {
  return { epoch: authEpoch, uid: activeSessionUid };
}

export function isAuthSessionCurrent(snapshot: AuthSessionSnapshot): boolean {
  return Boolean(snapshot.uid) && snapshot.epoch === authEpoch && snapshot.uid === activeSessionUid;
}

function clearUserJourneyStores() {
  try {
    sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_waitingReturnForCursoId');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_activeJornadaId');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_waitingReturnForVagaId');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('crasp_activeJornadaVagaId');
  } catch {}
  useJornadaCursosStore.setState({ jornadaCursos: [], hasFetched: false, loading: false });
  useJornadaVagasStore.setState({ jornadaVagas: [], hasFetched: false, loading: false });
}

export const useAuthStore = create<AuthState>((set) => ({
  user: null,
  profileComplete: null,
  userData: null,
  loading: true,
  isLoginOpen: false,
  totalUsuarios: null,
  totalMatriculas: null,
  totalDeCursos: null,
  totalDeVagas: null,

  setUser: (user) => {
    set({ user });
  },
  setProfileComplete: (complete) => set({ profileComplete: complete }),
  setUserData: (data) => set({ userData: data }),
  setLoading: (loading) => set({ loading }),
  setIsLoginOpen: (isOpen) => set({ isLoginOpen: isOpen }),
  setTotalUsuarios: (total) => set({ totalUsuarios: total }),

  signOut: async () => {
    authEpoch += 1;
    activeSessionUid = null;
    try { localStorage.removeItem('crasp_has_session'); } catch {}
    clearUserJourneyStores();
    useNavigationStore.setState({ activeTab: 'vitrine' });
    set({ user: null, profileComplete: null, userData: null, isLoginOpen: false, loading: false });

    const { auth } = await import('../lib/firebase');
    const { signOut: firebaseSignOut } = await import('firebase/auth');
    await firebaseSignOut(auth);
  },

  fetchTotais: async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/metadados/totais');
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`[AppSec] Resposta inválida de metadados: ${res.status}`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      set({ 
        totalUsuarios: data.totalUsuarios || 0,
        totalMatriculas: data.totalMatriculas || 0,
        totalDeCursos: data.totalDeCursos || 0,
        totalDeVagas: data.totalDeVagas || 0
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[AppSec] Erro ao buscar totais:", err);
    }
  },

  initializeAuth: () => {
    if (globalUnsubscribe || isInitializingAuth) return;
    isInitializingAuth = true;
    const myInitGen = ++initGeneration;

    import('../lib/firebase').then(({ auth }) => {
      if (myInitGen !== initGeneration || !isInitializingAuth) return;
      import('firebase/auth').then(({ onAuthStateChanged, signOut: firebaseSignOut }) => {
        if (myInitGen !== initGeneration || !isInitializingAuth || globalUnsubscribe) return;
        isInitializingAuth = false;
        globalUnsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (currentUser) => {
          if (myInitGen !== initGeneration) return;
          const myEpoch = ++authEpoch;
          const currentUid = currentUser?.uid ?? null;
          activeSessionUid = currentUid;

          clearUserJourneyStores();

          if (!currentUser || !currentUid) {
            try { localStorage.removeItem('crasp_has_session'); } catch {}
            useNavigationStore.setState({ activeTab: 'vitrine' });
            set({ user: null, profileComplete: null, userData: null, loading: false });
            return;
          }

          try { localStorage.setItem('crasp_has_session', '1'); } catch {}
          set({ user: currentUser, profileComplete: null, userData: null, loading: true });

          try {
            const token = await currentUser.getIdToken();
            if (myEpoch !== authEpoch || activeSessionUid !== currentUid) return;

            const res = await fetch('/api/usuarios/check-profile', {
              headers: {
                'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                'Cache-Control': 'no-store'
              }
            });
            if (myEpoch !== authEpoch || activeSessionUid !== currentUid) return;

            if (!res.ok) {
              throw new Error(`[AppSec] Resposta de erro do servidor: ${res.status}`);
            }

            const data = await res.json();
            if (myEpoch !== authEpoch || activeSessionUid !== currentUid) return;

            let userData = null;
            if (data.profileComplete) {
              userData = data.data;
              useJornadaCursosStore.setState({ jornadaCursos: userData?.jornadaCursos || [], hasFetched: true, loading: false });
              useJornadaVagasStore.setState({ jornadaVagas: userData?.jornadaVagas || [], hasFetched: true, loading: false });
            } else {
              clearUserJourneyStores();
            }

            set({
              user: currentUser,
              profileComplete: !!data.profileComplete,
              userData,
              loading: false
            });
          } catch (err) {
            if (myEpoch !== authEpoch || activeSessionUid !== currentUid) return;
            console.error("[AppSec] Falha na verificação de perfil:", err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Erro');
            try { localStorage.removeItem('crasp_has_session'); } catch {}
            clearUserJourneyStores();
            set({
              user: null,
              profileComplete: null,
              userData: null,
              loading: false
            });
            try {
              await firebaseSignOut(auth);
            } catch {}
          }
        });
      });
    });
  },

  cleanupAuth: () => {
    initGeneration += 1;
    authEpoch += 1;
    activeSessionUid = null;
    isInitializingAuth = false;
    if (globalUnsubscribe) {
      globalUnsubscribe();
      globalUnsubscribe = null;
    }
  }
}));
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useAuthStore.ts ---

import { create } from 'zustand';
import { CursoCatalogo } from '../types/catalog';
import { api } from '../api';

export type Curso = CursoCatalogo;

interface CursosState {
  cursos: Curso[];
  loading: boolean;
  fetchCursos: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const useCursosStore = create<CursosState>((set, get) => ({
  cursos: [],
  loading: false,
  fetchCursos: async () => {
    if (get().cursos.length > 0) return;
    set({ loading: true });
    try {
      const data = await api.cursos.listar();
      set({ cursos: Array.isArray(data) ? data : [], loading: false });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Erro ao buscar cursos:", err);
      set({ loading: false });
    }
  },
}));

  vagasDestaque: any[];

          instituicoes: data.instituicoes || [],

        });

        set({ loading: false });
      }

      console.error('Erro ao buscar pacote inicial (home-startup):', err);

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useJornadaCursosStore.ts ---
import { create } from 'zustand';

export interface JornadaCurso {
  id: string;
  cursoId: string;
  cursoSnapshot?: any;
  status: 'PRE_MATRICULADO' | 'MATRICULADO' | 'AGUARDANDO_CERTIFICADO' | 'EM_ANALISE' | 'CONCLUIDO' | 'DESISTIU';
  certificadoUrl?: string;
  avaliacao?: {
    notaCurso?: number;
    notaPlataforma?: number;
    notaMaterial?: number;
    comentario?: string;
  };
  createdAt?: any;
  updatedAt?: any;
}

interface JornadaCursosState {
  jornadaCursos: JornadaCurso[];
  loading: boolean;
  hasFetched: boolean;
  updateJornadaCursoLocal: (cursoId: string, newStatus: JornadaCurso['status'], extra?: Partial<JornadaCurso>) => void;
  addJornadaCursoLocal: (jornadaCurso: JornadaCurso) => void;
}

export const useJornadaCursosStore = create<JornadaCursosState>((set) => ({
  jornadaCursos: [],
  loading: false,
  hasFetched: false,
  updateJornadaCursoLocal: (cursoId, newStatus, extra) => {
    set((state) => ({
      jornadaCursos: state.jornadaCursos.map(j => (j.cursoId === cursoId ? { ...j, ...extra, status: newStatus } : j))
    }));
  },
  addJornadaCursoLocal: (jornadaCurso) => {
    set((state) => {

      if (state.jornadaCursos.some(j => j.cursoId === jornadaCurso.cursoId)) {
        return {
          jornadaCursos: state.jornadaCursos.map(j =>
            j.cursoId === jornadaCurso.cursoId ? jornadaCurso : j
          )
        };
      }
      return { jornadaCursos: [...state.jornadaCursos, jornadaCurso] };
    });
  }
}));
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useJornadaCursosStore.ts ---

  updatedAt?: any;
}

interface JornadaVagasState {
  jornadaVagas: JornadaVaga[];

  hasFetched: boolean;
  updateJornadaVagaLocal: (vagaId: string, newStatus: JornadaVaga['status']) => void;
  addJornadaVagaLocal: (jornadaVaga: JornadaVaga) => void;
}

export const useJornadaVagasStore = create<JornadaVagasState>((set) => ({
  jornadaVagas: [],
  loading: false,
  hasFetched: false,
  updateJornadaVagaLocal: (vagaId, newStatus) => {
    set((state) => ({
      jornadaVagas: state.jornadaVagas.map(c => (c.vagaId === vagaId ? { ...c, status: newStatus } : c))
    }));

    set((state) => {
      if (state.jornadaVagas.some(c => c.vagaId === jornadaVaga.vagaId)) {
        return {
          jornadaVagas: state.jornadaVagas.map(c =>
            c.vagaId === jornadaVaga.vagaId ? jornadaVaga : c
          )
        };
      }
      return { jornadaVagas: [...state.jornadaVagas, jornadaVaga] };
    });
  }
}));

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useMecStore.ts ---
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { IES, Campus, Curso } from '../types/schemas';
import { auth } from '../lib/firebase';

interface MecState {
  iesList: IES[];
  campusList: Campus[];
  cursoList: Curso[];
  loadingIes: boolean;
  loadingCampus: boolean;
  loadingCursos: boolean;
  fetchIesList: () => Promise<void>;
  fetchCampusList: (codIes: string) => Promise<void>;
  fetchCursoList: (codIes: string, codLocal: string, filtroGrau: string) => Promise<void>;
  clearCampusList: () => void;
  clearCursoList: () => void;
}

let iesReqId = 0;
let campusReqId = 0;
let cursoReqId = 0;

export const useMecStore = create<MecState>((set) => ({
  iesList: [],
  campusList: [],
  cursoList: [],
  loadingIes: false,
  loadingCampus: false,
  loadingCursos: false,

  fetchIesList: async () => {
    const reqId = ++iesReqId;
    set({ loadingIes: true });
    try {
      const user = auth.currentUser;
      const token = user ? await user.getIdToken() : '';
      
      const res = await fetch('/api/mec/ies', {
        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Servidor retornou status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      
      const data = await res.json();
      if (reqId !== iesReqId) return;
      
      if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        set({ iesList: data });
      } else {
        console.error("Payload e-MEC inválido (IES):", data);
        set({ iesList: [] });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (reqId !== iesReqId) return;
      console.error("Erro estrutural ao buscar IES:", err);
      set({ iesList: [] });
    } finally {
      if (reqId === iesReqId) {
        set({ loadingIes: false });
      }
    }
  },

  fetchCampusList: async (codIes) => {
    const reqId = ++campusReqId;
    set({ loadingCampus: true });
    try {
      const user = auth.currentUser;
      const token = user ? await user.getIdToken() : '';

      const res = await fetch(`/api/mec/campus/${encodeURIComponent(codIes)}`, {
        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Servidor retornou status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      
      const data = await res.json();
      if (reqId !== campusReqId) return;
      
      if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        set({ campusList: data });
      } else {
        console.error("Payload e-MEC inválido (Campus):", data);
        set({ campusList: [] });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (reqId !== campusReqId) return;
      console.error("Erro estrutural ao buscar Campus:", err);
      set({ campusList: [] });
    } finally {
      if (reqId === campusReqId) {
        set({ loadingCampus: false });
      }
    }
  },

  fetchCursoList: async (codIes, codLocal, filtroGrau) => {
    const reqId = ++cursoReqId;
    set({ loadingCursos: true });
    try {
      const user = auth.currentUser;
      const token = user ? await user.getIdToken() : '';

      const res = await fetch(`/api/mec/cursos/${encodeURIComponent(codIes)}/${encodeURIComponent(codLocal)}?filtroGrau=${encodeURIComponent(filtroGrau)}`, {
        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Servidor retornou status: ${res.status}`);
      }
      
      const data = await res.json();
      if (reqId !== cursoReqId) return;
      
      if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        set({ cursoList: data });
      } else {
        console.error("Payload e-MEC inválido (Cursos):", data);
        set({ cursoList: [] });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (reqId !== cursoReqId) return;
      console.error("Erro estrutural ao buscar Cursos:", err);
      set({ cursoList: [] });
    } finally {
      if (reqId === cursoReqId) {
        set({ loadingCursos: false });
      }
    }
  },

  clearCampusList: () => {
    campusReqId++;
    set({ campusList: [], loadingCampus: false });
  },
  clearCursoList: () => {
    cursoReqId++;
    set({ cursoList: [], loadingCursos: false });
  },
}));
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useMecStore.ts ---

--- INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useVagasStore.ts ---
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { VagaCatalogo } from '../types/catalog';

export type Vaga = VagaCatalogo;

interface VagasStore {
  vagas: Vaga[];
  loading: boolean;
  fetchVagas: () => Promise<void>;
  }

export const useVagasStore = create<VagasStore>((set, get) => ({
  vagas: [],
  loading: false,
  fetchVagas: async () => {
    if (get().vagas.length > 0) return;
    set({ loading: true });
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/vagas');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (Array.isArray(data)) {
          const vagasComId = data.map((v: any) => ({ ...v, id: v.codigoDaVaga || Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9) }));
          set({ vagas: vagasComId, loading: false });
        } else {
          set({ vagas: [], loading: false });
        }
      } else {
        console.error('Erro ao buscar vagas.');
        set({ loading: false });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Erro na requisição de vagas:', error);
      set({ loading: false });
    }
  },
}));
--- FIM DO ARQUIVO: /src/store/useVagasStore.ts ---
